MUKTABODHA INDOLOGICAL RESEARCH INSTITUTE and INSTITUT FRANCAIS DE PONDICHERY


Catalog number : I00017
Uniform title: bodhāyanaprayoga
Manuscript : IFP manuscript 30720
Description:
This text contrains the procedure for performing purvaprayoga and aparaprayoga. According to the rules of Bodhayanaha. Purvaprayoga and Aparaprayoga are the domestic ritual which as householder is to be performed by offering oblations into the holy fire. The size of palm leaf is 17 cm length and 2 cm width and the bundle is in good in condition. There are 5 to 6 lines in each folio. The text deals with snānam prāṇāyāmaḥ, śrāddhaprayoga, gṛhasthasyaupāsanam| vidhuraupāsanavidhiḥ, brahmayajñasya mukhyakālaḥ, bhūtaśuddhiḥ, mahanyāsam| ātmasaṃpuṭīkaraṇam, raudrīkaraṇam|, śivasaṅkalpam|, śivasaṅkalpam|| pañcabrahmanyāsa,
aṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsam|, pañcākṣaranyāsaḥ śrīkaṇṭhamantra(mātṛ)kānyāsaḥ|, pitṛyajñam| bhūtayajñam| manuṣyayajñam| brahmayajñam/ ṛṣiyajñam, atha bhojanavidhiḥ, suputrakāmastvapararātre svadāramaithunaṃ kuryāt| brahmacāridharmaḥ|, niṣekaviṣayaḥ| brahmacāridharmaḥ brahmacāridharmaḥ|, snātakadharmaḥ| dviyajñopavītī| gṛhasthadharmaḥ| tailābhyaṅgavidhiḥ, kṣaurakālaḥ, kṣauravidhiḥ, strīdharmaḥ, vidhavādharma śubhamastu bodhāyanamantrapraśnam|, prathamāśvāsaḥ ||1|| dvitīyyāśvāsaḥ||2|| samāvartanam, tritīyyāśvāsaḥ| caturthāśvāsaḥ, pañcamāśvāsaḥ, śakunasūktam, saptamāśvāsaḥ, aṣṭamāśvāsaḥ, navamāśvāsaḥ|| daśamavāsaḥ |

vande vināyakaṃ devaṃ …madevaṃ śivāṃ śubham|

daṇḍādisahito gṛhānnisṛtyā(ya) grāmāt bahirdeśaṃ gatvā daṇḍādīnekatra nikṣipyā(ya) naiṛtādyanupayuktasthalaṃ gatvā|

visṛjet| tataḥ kāṣṭādinā gudamunmṛjyā(ya) vāmapāṇinā śiśnaṃ saṃgṛhya itareṇa pūrvagṛhītamṛttikāṃ gṛhītvā 
snānādyanupayuktajalatīraṃ gatvā tathā (l6.) śaucaṃ kuryāt| tadabhāve jale mṛttikāṃ gṛhītvā samīpe nikṣipyā(ya) tat

sarasvatīmantrasya śabdabrahma ṛṣiḥ| devīgāyatrīcchandaḥ| sabindumātṛkā sarasvatī devatā| (l2.) aṃ bījaṃ āṃ śaktiḥ saṃ

ñaṃ īṃ tasthanibhāṃ namaḥ| uṃ ṭaṃ ṭhaṃ ḍaṃ ḍhaṃ ṇaṃ ūṃ (l3.) madhyamā eṃ taṃ thaṃ daṃ dhaṃ naṃ aiṃ anāmikā| oṃ paṃ phaṃ baṃ bhaṃ maṃ oṃ 
kaniṣṭhikā| aṃ yaṃ raṃ laṃ vaṃ śaṃ ṣaṃ saṃ haṃ āḥ karatalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ| evaṃ vinyāsaḥ| 

sandūrakāntimamitābharaṇāṃ triṇetrāṃ *vidyākṣasūtramṛgapotavaraṃ dadhānām|* (l4.)
pārśvasthitoṃ bhagavatimatikāñcanābhāṃ dhyāyet karābjadhṛtapustakavarṇamālām||

pañcāśavarṇābhadervihatavadanadoḥ pāda*yukkukṣipakṣo* (l5.)
dehāmbhāsvat kapardā kalitaśaśikalāmindukundāvadātām|
akṣasṛkkumbhacittālikhitakaravarakarāṃ trikṣaṇāṃ 
padmasaṃsthāmacchākalpāmakṛcchastana*jaghanabharāṃ bhāti tāṃ namāmi||* (p14b., f12a., l1.)

aṃ namaḥ śirasi| āṃ namaḥ mukhavṛtte| iṃ namaḥ dakṣiṇanetre| īṃ namaḥ vāmanetre| uṃ namaḥ dakṣiṇakarṇe| ūṃ namaḥ vāmakarṇe| ṛṃ 
namaḥ dakṣiṇanāsāpuṭe| ṛṃ namaḥ vāmanāsāpuṭe| ḷṃ namaḥ dakṣiṇa(l2.) gaṇḍe| ḹṃ namaḥ vāmagaṇḍe| eṃ namaḥ uttaroṣṭhe| aiṃ 
namaḥ adharoṣṭhe| oṃ namaḥ uttaradantapaṅktau| auṃ namaḥ adharadantapaṅktau| aṃ namaḥ mūrdhni| aḥ namaḥ kaṇṭhe| kaṃ namaḥ 
dakṣiṇabhuje| khaṃ namaḥ dakṣiṇa (l3.)korpare| gaṃ namaḥ dakṣiṇabandhe(maṇibandhe)| ghaṃ namaḥ dakṣahaste| ṅaṃ namaḥ dakṣāṅguliṣu| caṃ 
namaḥ vāmabhuje| chaṃ namaḥ vāmakorpare| jaṃ namaḥ vāmamaṇibandhe| jhaṃ namaḥ vāmahaste| ñaṃ namaḥ vāmāṅguli| … ṭaṃ namaḥ 
dakṣo(l4.)rumūle| ṭhaṃ namaḥ dakṣajānunī| ḍaṃ namaḥ dakṣajaṅghāgre| ḍhaṃ namaḥ dakṣapāde| ṇaṃ namaḥ dakṣapādāṅuguliṣu| taṃ namaḥ 
vāmorumūle| thaṃ namaḥ vāmajānuni| daṃ namaḥ vāmajaṅghāgre| dhaṃ namaḥ vāmapāde| naṃ namaḥ (l5.) vāmapādāṅguliṣu| paṃ namaḥ 
dakṣiṇapārśve| phaṃ namaḥ vāmapārśve| baṃ namaḥ pṛṣṭhe| bhaṃ namaḥ nābhau| maṃ namaḥ jaṭhare| yaṃ namaḥ yagātmane namaḥ hṛdaye| raṃ 
asṛgātmane namaḥ dakṣiṇadormūle| laṃ māṃsātmane namaḥ vāmadormūle| (p15a., f12b., l1.) vaṃ devātmane namaḥ aparadale| 
śaṃ asthyātmane namaḥ dakṣiṇakakṣe| ṣaṃ majjātmane namaḥ| vāmakakṣe| saṃ śuklātmane namaḥ dakṣapāde| haṃ prāṇātmane namaḥ 
vāmapāde| laṃ namaḥ sapādādijaṭhare| kṣaṃ namaḥ mukhādijaṭhare| iti nyastvā| (l2.) mātṛkā caiva vidmahe sarasvatyai ca dhīmahi| 
tanno vāṇi pracodayāt| iti daśavāraṃ japet| kānanavṛttaca(dva)kṣiśrutino bhaṇḍoṣṭhadantamūrdhāsye doṃpat? sandhyagreṣu ca 
pārśvadvayapṛṣṭhanābhi(l3.)jaṭhareṣu hṛddormūlāparagalakakṣeṣu hṛdādipāṇipādayuge jaṭharānanayo vyāpakasaṃjñā nyasedadhākṣarān kramaśaḥ| 
evaṃ ādimanyāsaṃ kṛtvā| paścāt gāyatrijapasya siddhirbhavet| …(l4.) bhāradvājaṃ upasthāya namaskuryāt catuḥ 
sandhyādidevatāḥ| sandhyāpurastān sāvitrī gāyatrī ca sarasvatī| etāḥ sandhyādayaḥ proktāścatasro devatāḥ kramāt| sva sva 
nāma caturthyantaṃ pramavādi(l5.)namontakam| mantramāsāmiha proktaṃ praṇamet sva sva mantrataḥ| sarvābhyo devatābhyaścetyetat 
praṇavasaṃpuṭam| uktvā namo nama iti praṇamet sarvadevatāḥ| (p15b., f13a., sideheading samidhādhānam| l1.) 
kāmokārṣīḥ manyutakārṣī namo nama ityapi| uktvā pradakṣiṇenaiva deviṃ vipro'bhivādayet| karṇayugmaṃ svahastābhyāṃ spṛṣṭvā 
jānudvayādikam| caraṇāṅguṣṭhaparyantaṃ saṃmṛjya tu śanaiḥ śanaiḥ| abhivādya tu gāyatrīṃ (l2.) namaskuryādīśastataḥ| prāci ca 
dakṣiṇā caiva pratīcī cottarordhvakā adharā cāntarikṣā ca … bhūmiścāṣṭoditā diśaḥ etāḥ pradakṣiṇenaiva praṇamet sva sva 
mantrataḥ| yama-viṣṇu-virūpākṣa(l3.)-savitṝṇāmupasthitam| kuryānnaliṅgakaiḥ mantraiḥ dvijo yāmyādidiṅmukhaḥ| evaṃ 
sandhyāmupāsyātha pitarāya(yā)grajān gurūn| tato'gramahaṃ praviśet| sajalaṃ prokṣaṇimādāya (l4.) gṛhamāgatya pādau 
prakṣālyācamya| gṛhaṃ prokṣya| brahmacāriṇaḥ samidhādhānam| śucau deśe gomayena caturaśramupalipya| tatra prāṅmukha upaviśyācamya| 
mama upātta + (l5.) prītyarthaṃ prātassamidhādhānaṃ kariṣye| ignimupasamādhāya| devasyatvā savituḥ prasave'śvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo 
hastābhyāṃ iti pūrve paristriṇāmi bhūrbhuvassuvariti dakṣiṇe paristṛṇāmi| udvayatamasaspari (p16a., f13b., l1.) 
paśyanto jyotiruttaraṃ devaṃ devatrā sūryamaganamajyotiruttamamiti paścime paristṛṇāmi| agna āyāhi vītaye gṛṇāno 
havyadātaye nihotā satsi barhiṣītyuttare paristṛṇāmi| vidyudasi vidya me pāpmānamṛtām| juṣasvanaḥ (l2.) samidhamagne| 
adyaśauca| bṛhadyajitam| dhūmramṛṇvan upaspṛśadivyaṃ sānastūpaiḥ saguṃraśmibhiḥ tadanu sūryasya| aditenumanyasva 
anumatenumanyasva sarasvatenumanyasva devasavitaḥ prasūva samidāhutiḥ| (l3.) bhū svāhā agnaya idam,  bhuva svāhā vāyava 
idam, suva svāhā sūryāyedam, oṃ bhūrbhuvassuvassvāhā prajāpataya idam| vṛṣṭirasi vṛśca me pāpmānam| juṣasvanaḥ + sūryasya| 
aditenava magusthāḥ| (l4.) anumatenvamagusthāḥ sarasvatenumagusthāḥ devasavitaḥ prāsāvīḥ| svāheti samidhamādhāya upasthānam| 
utthāya| yatte agne tejastenāhaṃ tejasvī bhūyāsam, yatte agnau varcastenāhaṃ varcasvī (l5.)bhūyāsaṃ yatte agne harastenāhaṃ 
harasvī bhūyāsam, mayi medhāṃ mayi prajāṃ mayyagnistojo dadhātu, mayi medhāṃ mayi prajāṃ mayindra indriyaṃ dadhātu, mayi medhāṃ 
mayi prajāṃ mayi sūryo (p16b., f14a., sideheading gṛhasthaupāsanam, l1.) bhrājo dadhātu agnena supathārāye 
asmānviśvāni deva vayunāni vi….| yuyodhyasmajjuhurāṇameno bhūyiṣṭhānte nama uktiṃ vidhema| agnaye namaḥ| mantrahīnaṃ 
kriyāhīnaṃ bhaktihīnaṃ hutāśana| yaddhutaṃ tu (l2.) mayā deva paripūrṇaṃ tadastu te| prāyaścittānyaśeṣāṇi tapaḥ karmātmakāni vai| 
yāni teṣāmaśeṣāṇāṃ śrī śivānusmaraṇaṃ param| namaskṛtya| abhivādaye + bhoḥ| (l3.) bhasmaṃ samādāya| mānastoke tanaye 
māna āyuṣi māno goṣu māno aśveṣurīriṣaḥ| vīrānmāno rudrabhāmito'vadhīrhaviṣmanto ramasā vidhema te| ityabhimantryā|
(l4.) medhāvī bhūyāsamiti lalāṭe| tejasvī bhūyāsamiti hṛdaye| varcasvī bhūyāsamiti dakṣiṇāṃse| brahmavarcasī bhūyāsamiti 
vāmāṃse| āyuṣmān bhūyāsamiti śirasi| svastyaṃ (l5.) netre| … muccāraṇam| anantaraṃ śuddhācamanam| 

atha gṛhasthasyaupāsanam| 
pādau prakṣālyācamya śucau deśe upaviśya prāṇānāyamya| dhārya pakṣe'gnimupasamā…(dhā)ya saṅkalpaḥ| (p17a., f14b., 
l1.) samitsamāropaṇapakṣe laukikāgnimupasamādhāya samidagnivisarjanam| ājuhvānaḥ supratīkaḥ purastādagne svāṃ 
yonimāsīdasādhya| asmin sadhasthe adhyuttarasmin viśvedevā yajamānaśca sīdata| (l2.) iti samidhamādhāya| 
udbuddhisvāgne pratijāgṛhyenamiṣṭāpūrte saṃsṛje dhāmayaṃ ca punaḥ kṛṇvaṃstvāpitaraṃ yuvānamanvātāguṃsi tvayi tantu me taṃ iti 
prārthya| saṅkalpaḥ| mama upātta (l3.) durita-kṣayadvāra-parameśvaraprītyarthaṃ prātaraupāsanahomaṃ hoṣyāmi| apa upa spṛśya| 
agnerbhagavato mahāmantrasya agastyabhagavān ṛṣiḥ| anuṣṭup chandaḥ| hutāśano (l4.) devatā| raṃ bījam| svāhā śaktiḥ| maṃ 
kīlakam| mama sarvābhīṣṭasidhyarthe aupāsane viniyogaḥ| svasti pūrṇamukhātmane aṅgu(ṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ)| darśapūrṇātmane 
tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ| (l5.) cāturmāsyātmane madhyamā(bhyāṃ namaḥ)| dhūmrākṣāya anāmi(kābhyāṃ namaḥ)| sahasraśīrṣe 
kaniṣṭhikā(bhyāṃ namaḥ)| saptajihvātmane karatala(karapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ)| evamaṅganyāsaḥ| 
dhyānam -
agnerdhyānaṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvahomārtha*siddhaye|* (p17b., f15a., l1.) 
dvimukhaṃ caikahṛdayaṃ catuśro(śśro)traṃ dvināsikam||
āsyadvayaṃ ca ṣaṇṇetraṃ piṅgalaṃ saptahastakam|
trimekhalaṃ tripādaṃ ca saptajihvā samāyutam||
catuśri(śśṛ)ṅgaṃ vṛṣārūḍhaṃ bālādityasamaprabham|
savyabhāge caturhastaṃ *trihastamapasavyake||* (l2.)
sṛksṛvau cākṣamālāṃ ca śakti dakṣiṇake kare|
tomaraṃ vyajanaṃ caiva ghṛtapātraṃ tu vāmake||
dadhānamātmābhimukha(khaṃ) dhyāyeddevaṃ hutāśanam|
catvāri śri(śṛ)ṅgā trayo'sya *pāde dve śīrṣe saptahastā so asya||* (l3.)
tridhā baddho vṛṣabho roravīti mahodevo martyāṃ āviveśa|
eṣa hi devaḥ pradiśo'nusarvāḥ pūrvo hi jātaḥ sugarbhe antaḥ||
javijāya mānaḥ sajaniṣyamāṇaḥ pratyaṅmukhāstiṣṭhati viśvato mukhaḥ|* (l4.)
agnipūjana agnau pūrvādidikṣu| agnaye namaḥ| jātavedase namaḥ| sahaujase namaḥ| ajirāprabhave namaḥ| vaiśvānarāya namaḥ| 
nayyāpase namaḥ| pañcaktirāyase namaḥ| (l5.) visarpiṇe namaḥ| madhye yajñapuruṣāya namaḥ| ityabhyarcyā(rcya)| juṣasvanaḥ + 
sūryasya| paristaraṇam| devasyatvetyādi barhiṣītyantaṃ uktvā paritaḥ paristīrya| aditenu manyasva anumatenu manyasva 
sarasvatenumanyasva devasavi(p18a., f15b., l1.)tu prasuva| indrāya namaḥ| yamāya namaḥ| varuṇāya namaḥ| somāya namaḥ| 
agnaye namaḥ| niṝtaye namaḥ| vāyave namaḥ| īśānāya namaḥ| agnaye namaḥ| ātmane namaḥ| sarvebhyo brāhmaṇebhyo namaḥ| 
ityabhyarcya vidyudasi vidya… pāpmānam| agnau (l2.) samidādhāya hoṣyāmīti sadasyān prārthya| jahudhīti tadanujñāṃ 
labdhvā| sūryāya svāhā sūryāyedaṃ na mama| iti madhye| uttarāhutiḥ kiñcidadhikā prajāpataye svāhā prajāpataya idaṃ na mama 
iti kiñcidīśāne| (l3.) āhutidvayaṃ saṃsṛṣṭaṃ cet āhutisahasrāṣṭaprāyaścittārthaṃ vanaspatihomaṃ hoṣyāmi| yatra vettha 
vanaspate devānāṃ guhyā nāmāni tatra havyāni kāmaya iti samidhamādhā(dā)ya pūrvavat (l4.) amantraka āhudva(tidva)yaṃ 
kuryāt| vṛṣṭirasi vṛśca me …pmānaṃ juṣasvanaḥ + sūryasya| aditenvagamusthāḥ anumatenvamagusthāḥ sarasvatenvamagusthāḥ| 
devasavitaḥ prāsāvīḥ samidhamādhā(dā)ya| (l5.) upasthānam| yatte agne + vidhema| agnaye namaḥ mantra….smaraṇaṃ … 
namaskṛtya abhivādaye + ….. samitsamāropaṇapakṣe mantraḥ| ayaṃ neyo niṝtviyo yato jāto arocathāḥ| (p18b., 
f16a., sideheading sāyamaupāsanākaraṇaprāyaścittam l1.) taṃ jānannagna ārohādhānovardha…..greṇāgniṃ gṛhītvā … 
samāya bṛhatsāmekṣatrabhracraddhavṛṣṇiyaṃ triṣṭubhaujaśśubhitamugravīraṃ indrastomena pañcadaśena madhyamidaṃ vāte sagareṇa rakṣā| (l2.) 
iti lalāṭe dhṛtva(tvā)| śuddhācamanaṃ kuryāt| ātmasamāropaṇapakṣe| ātmāgnivisasthānamantram| upāvaroha jātavedaḥ 
punastvandevebhyo hasma vṛhatu prajānan| āyuḥ prajāṃ (l3.) rayimasāsunayehyajasrodidi(ti) hīno du………ṇa| iti 
laukikāgnāvātmasthaṃ visṛjya| pūrvavat kuryāt| ātmasamāropaṇamantraḥ| yā te agne yajñiyātanūsya 
yehyārohā(l4.)tātmānam| acchāvasūni kṛṇvannasmai nayyāghrā imaṇi yajño bhūtvā yajñamāsitasvāṃ yoniṃ jātavedā bhuva 
ājāyamānaḥ sakṣaya ehi| iti jvaladagniṃ ātmāni gṛhītva śeṣaṃ (l5.)pūrvavat kuryāt| sāyamaupāsanākaraṇe pūrvedyuḥ 
sāyamaupāsanena saha adyaprātaraupāsanahomaṃ hoṣyāmīti saṃkalpya| pradhānāhuteḥ| pūrvamakaraṇaprāyaścittamayāścāgne (p19a., 
f16b., sideheading bhuktaprāyaścittam, vidhunaupāsanam l1.) nakabhi …. bṛhtyamitvamayā asi| agrasā 
manasādhṛto'yasā havyamūhiṣe yānothehi bheṣajaṃ svāhā| agnaye ayasa idaṃ na mama| ityājyena tadabhāve samidhā vā hutvā| 
samidhamādhā(dā)yā(ya) sāyamāhutidvayaṃ kṛtvā (l2.) prātarāhutidvayaṃ kāryam| rātrāvajñānato bhuktaṃ cet prātaḥ 
akaraṇaprāyaścittaṃ kṛtvā| bhuktaprāyaścittaṃ kariṣye| iti saṃkalpyā(ya)| ājyāhutiḥ tadabhāve samidhaḥ| yadvo vayaṃ pramināma 
vratāni viduṣāṃ devā(l3.) aviduṣṭarāsaḥ| agniṣṭadvigumāpraṇāti vidvān yebhirdevālaṃ ṛtubhiḥ kalpayāti| prātastathā cet| 
snātvā| bhuktaprāyaścittaṃ kariṣye iti saṃkalpya| yatte vayaṃ puruṣatrāyaviṣṭhāvidvāṃsaśca(l4.)kṛmā-kaccanāgaṃtuyiṣva smāṃ 
aditeranāgā….nāmasiśiśratho viṣvagagne| iti vicārya kāryam| vidhuraupāsanavidhiḥ| laukikāgni 
praṇavenoddhṛtyānvagnirityāhṛtya praṣṭo divīti (l5.) nidhāya catuḥ śakalaiḥ homam| sat savituḥ + dadyāt| tāṃ 
savituvareṇyasya citrāmahaṃ vṛṇe sumati| viśvajanyāṃ yāmasya kaṇvo aduhat prapināṃ sahasradhārāṃ payasāmahiṅgāḥ| viśvā(l6.) 
nideva savitaḥ + suva| adyāno devasavitaḥ + saviśeṣaṃ pūrvavat| āropaṇāvaropaṇena staḥ| ullekhanaṃ ca| evaṃ nityaṃ kuryāt| 
homānantaram| samit-puṣpa-darbhaharaṇakālaḥ| (p19b., f17a., sideheading samidādiharaṇam, mādhyāhnikam| l1.) 
yatheṣṭam āyurbalaṃ yaśovarca + vanaspata ityuktvā … nidāssaraṇa iṣṭadevasmaraṇaṃ kṛtvā puṣpābharaṇaṃ darbhābharaṇam| 
viriñcanāsikotpaṇaparameṣṭhiparigraha| nudapāpāni sarvāṇi darbhasvastikanomaḥ| tataḥ (l2.)adhyayanādikaṃ kuryāt| tataḥ 
bhojanasāmagri saṃpādanīyam| saṃpādyaikādaśanāḍiṣu gatā….yatha mādhyāhnikaṃ kuryāt| mṛdādi saṃbhārān gṛhītvā| 
pūrvavannadyādikaṃ gatvā pavitrapāṇiḥ saṃkalpya (l3.) pūrvavat snātvā| ārdravastraṃ parityajya śuklavastraṃ paridhāya uttarīyamapi 
dhṛtva(vā)| pādau prakṣālyācamyārdravastraṃ brahmayajñānte niṣpīḍayet| pūrvavadvibhūtidhāraṇaṃ kṛtvā saṃkalpya| pūrvamāpo(l4.) 
hiṣṭhādi navaprokṣaṇa….kṛtvā| āpaḥpunantu pṛthivīṃ pṛthvīpūtā punantu māṃ punantu brahmaṇaspatirbrahmapūtā punātu māṃ 
yaducchiṣṭamabhojyaṃ yadvā duścaritaṃ mama sarvaṃ punantu māmāpo (l5.) satāṃ ca pratigraha svāhā| pūrvavadācamya| 
dadhikrāviṇṇodibhirmārjayitva(tvā) pradakṣiṇaṃ pariṣicya| ṛjukāyaḥ arghyamekaṃ pūrvavat prāyaścittārghyaṃ ca astropasaṃhāraṃ ca 
kṛtvā| pūrvavat pradakṣiṇam| (p20a., f17b., l1.) ādityāditarpaṇaṃ kṛtvāācamya| vandanamapi kṛtvā japasthānam 
āgatya namaskṛtyāsīnaḥ prāṅmukhaḥ pūrvavat prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvā gāyatrimāvāhyāṣṭottaraśatamurassamaṃ japtvā| prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvā| 
gāyatrimudvāsya| (l2.) utthāyāsatmejyādimantrasya kavyādayo ṛṣayaḥ triṣṭubādīni chandāṃsi bhāskaro devatā sūryopasthāne 
viniyogaḥ| ūrdhvabāhu vyomamudrayā sūryaṃ paśyan japet| āsatyena rajasā vartamāno niveśaya(l3.)nnamṛtaṃ martyaṃ ca hira….tho

asmin mahatyarṇava iti śirasi| asmin mahatyarṇaventarikṣe (l2.) + dhi| teṣāṃ + tanmasi| śirasi bhavetyonnamaḥ| 
sasrāṇīti lalāṭe| sahasrāṇi sahasraśo ye + tanma… lalāṭe rudrebhyo namaḥ| haṃsaḥ śuciṣaditi bhruvormadhye| haṃsaḥ suciṣat + 
bṛhat| (l3.)bhra(bhru)vormadhye haṃsāya namaḥ| triyambakaṃ yajāmaha iti netratrayoḥ| triyambakaṃ + māmṛtāt| netrayostriyambakāya 
namaḥ| nama sṛtyāyeti ca karṇayoḥ| nama sṛtyāya ca + vaiśantāya ca| karṇayoḥ sṛtyā hi rūpāya (l4.) rudrāya namaḥ| 
mānastoka iti nāsikāyām| mānastoke + mate| nāsikāyāṃ rudrāya namaḥ| avatatyeti mukhe| avatatya dhanustvaṃ + bhava| 
mukhe sahasrākṣāya namaḥ| nīlagrīvau dvau kaṇṭhe| nīlagrīvāḥ śitikaṇṭhā + carāḥ| teṣāṃ (l5.) sahasra + tanmasi| kaṇṭhe 
nīlagrīvebhyo namaḥ| nīlagrīvāḥ śitikaṇṭhā divaṃ + tāḥ| teṣāṃ + tanmasi| upakaṇṭhe nīlagrīvebhyo namaḥ| namaste 
astvāyudhāyeti bāhvoḥ| namaste astvāyudhāyā + dhanvane| (p27a., f24b., l1.) bāhvorāyudhabāhudhanubhyo namaḥ| yāte 
hetirityupabāhvoḥ| yāte hetiḥ + bhuja| upabāhvorhetaye namaḥ| ye tīrthānīti hastayoḥ| ye tīrthāni + giṇaḥ| teṣāṃ + 
tanmasi| haste yo rudrebhyo namaḥ| sadyojātamiti (l2.) pañcānuvākān pañcasvaṅguliṣu vinyasya| 
aṅguṣṭhayostarjanībhyām| sadyojātaṃ + bhavāya namaḥ| aṅguṣṭhayoḥ sadyojātāya namaḥ| tarjanyādiṣvaṅguṣṭhābhyām| vāmadevāya 
+ nmanāya namaḥ| tarjanyorvāmadevāya namaḥ| aghorebhyo(l3.) + rūpebhyaḥ| madhyamayoraghorāya namaḥ| tatpuruṣāya + dayāt| 
anāmikayostatpuruṣāya namaḥ| īśānaḥ sarvavidyānāṃ + śivom| kaniṣṭhikayorīśānāya namaḥ| namo vaḥ kirikebhya iti hṛdaye| 
(l4.) namo vaḥ kirike + bhyaḥ| hṛdaye kirikādi hṛdayebhyo namaḥ| namo gaṇebhya iti pṛṣṭhe| namo gaṇebhyo + namaḥ pṛṣṭhe 
gaṇābhyo namaḥ| namo hiraṇyabāhava iti pārśvayoḥ| namo hiraṇyabāhave + (l5.)paśūnāṃ pataye namaḥ| pārśvayorhiraṇyabāhave 
namaḥ| vijyandhanuriti jaṭhare| vijyandhanuḥ + gaḍiḥ| jaṭhare dhanurādibhyo namaḥ| īḍyaścetyudare| īḍyaścāsivandya + 
jātavedāḥ| udare (p27b., f25a., l1.)agnaye namaḥ| hiraṇyagarbha iti nābhau| hiraṇyagarbha + vi… nābhau hiraṇyagarbhāya 
namaḥ| mīḍhuṣṭameti kaṭyām| mīḍhuṣṭaḥ + gahi| kaṭyām| śivatamāya namaḥ| ye bhūtānāmadhipataya iti guhye| ye bhūtānāmadhi 
+ naḥ| teṣāṃ + nmasi| …hye bhūtapatibhyo (l2.) namaḥ| ye anneṣvityaṇḍayoḥ| ye anneṣu + nān teṣāṃ + nmasi| 
aṇḍayo rudrebhyo namaḥ| saśirājāta…..dā ityapāne| saśirājātavedāḥ| akṣaraṃ paramaṃ padam| vedānāguṃ śira uttamam|

soma + mānaḥ| jaṅghayosaṃsṛṣṭajite namaḥ| viśvaṃ bhūtamiti gulphayoḥ| viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ bhuvanaṃ citra + astu| gulphayoḥ rudrāya 
namaḥ| ye phathāmīti pādayoḥ| ye pathāṃ + vyudhaḥ| teṣāṃ (l5.) + nmasi| pādayoḥ| rudrebhyo namaḥ| adhyavocadadhikavacam|

ityastram| pramuñca + vapa| bhagavate astrāya namaḥ| ya etāvantaśceti digbandhaḥ| ya etāvantaśca + (l2.) tanmasi| 
digbhyo rudrebhyo namaḥ| onnamo bhagavate rudrāyeti namaskāraṃ nyaset| oṅkāraṃ mūrdhni vinyasya| oṃ namaḥ mūrdhni| nakāraṃ 
nāsikāgrataḥ| nannamaḥ nāsikāgre mokāraṃ tu lalāṭe vai| monnamaḥ| lalāṭe| bhakāraḥ mukhamadhyataḥ| bhaṃ namaḥ mukha(l3.)madhye| 
gakāraṃ kaṇṭhadeśe tu| gaṃ namaḥ kaṇṭhadeśe| vakāraṃ hṛdi vinyaset| vaṃ namaḥ hṛdaye| tekāraṃ dakṣiṇe haste| teṃ namaḥ| dakṣiṇahaste| 
rukāraṃ vāmato nyaset| ruṃ namaḥ| vāmahaste| drākāraṃ nābhideśe tu| (l4.) drāṃ namaḥ| nābhyām| yākāraṃ pādayo nyaset| 
….namaḥ| pādayoḥ| sadyaṃ ca pādayornyasya| sadyojātaṃ + vāya namaḥ| pādayoḥ sadyojātāya namaḥ| vāmanyasyorumadhyataḥ| 
vāmadevāya + namaḥ nāya namaḥ| (l5.) ūrvorvāmadevāya namaḥ| aghoraṃ hṛdi vinyasya| aghorebhyo + rūpebhyaḥ| hṛdaye aghorāya 
namaḥ| mukhe tatpuruṣaṃ nyaset| tatpuruṣā + dadhāt| mukhe tatpuruṣāya namaḥ| īśānaṃ mūrdhni vinyasya| (p28b., f26a., 
sideheading haṃsagāyatri, dik saṃpuṭīkaraṇam|, l1.) īśānassarvavidyānāṃ + śivom| mūrdhni īśānā…..so nāma 
sadāśivaḥ| haṃsa-haṃseti yo brūyāddhaṃso nāma sadāśivaḥ| asya śrī haṃsagāyatrīmahāmantrasya| ātma ṛṣiḥ| avyaktagāyatrīcchandaḥ| 
paramahaṃso devatā| haṃ bījam| (l2.) saḥ śaktiḥ sohaṃ kīlakam| mama haṃsagāyatrīprasādasidhyarthe jape viniyogaḥ| haṃ sāṃ 
aṅgu| haṃ siṃ tarja| haṃ sūṃ madhya| haṃ saiṃ| anāmi| haṃ sauṃ kani| haṃ saḥ karatala| evamaṅgavinyāsaḥ| oṃ bhūrbhuvassuvaromiti 
(l3.) digbandhaḥ| 
dhyānam -
gamāgamasthaṃ gamanādiśūnyaṃ  … dhūpadīpaṃ timirāndhakāram|
	paśyāmi taṃ sarvajanāntarasthaṃ namāmi haṃsaṃ paramātmarūpam||
haṃsahaṃsāḥ paramahaṃsāḥ so'haṃ haṃsaḥ|
	haṃsahaṃsāya *vidmahe paramahaṃsāya dhīmahi||* (l4.)
tanno haṃsapracodayāt|

evaṃ nyāsavidhiṃ kṛtvā tataḥ saṃpuṭamārabhet trātāramindraṃ tvanno agne sugaṃ naḥ panthāmasunvantam
tatvāyāmyāno niyugbhirvayaguṃsomamīśāna(l5.) masmairudrāsyonāpṛthivītyetat saṃpuṭamindrādidikṣu vinyasya| ādau 
pramavamuccāryā(rya) vyāhṛtiḥ praṇavānvitam| bījaṃ mantraṃ samuccārya mantrānte bījamācaret| praṇavaṃ vyāhṛtiṃ caiva tataḥ 
praṇavamācaret| (l6.) oṃ bhūrbhuvassuvaḥ| oṃ trātāramindraṃ + tvindraḥ| oṃ bhūrbhuvassuvaḥ| oṃ pūrvasyāṃ diśi indrāya namaḥ| oṃ 
bhū + vaḥ| oṃ naṃ tvanno agne + smat| naṃ bhū + vaḥ| naṃ tvanno agne + …dhyasmat| naṃ bhūrbhuvassuvaḥ| (p29a., 
f26b., l1.) oṃ dakṣiṇapūrvasyāṃ diśi agnaye namaḥ| oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ suvaḥ| moṃ sugaṃ naḥ panthām + yajāḥ| moṃ bhūrbhuvassuvaḥ| 
oṃ dakṣimasyāṃ diśi yamāya namaḥ| oṃ bhū + vaḥ| bhaṃ asunvantaḥ ya + mastu| bhaṃ oṃ bhū + vaḥ| dakṣiṇaparasyāṃ diśi 
niṝtaye namaḥ| (l2.) oṃ bhū + vaḥ| gaṃ tatvāyāmi + mokṣaḥ| gaṃ bhū + vaḥ| om aparasyāṃ diśi varuṇāya namaḥ| oṃ 
bhū + vaḥ| vaṃ āno niyugbhiḥ + ssadānaḥ| vaṃ bhūrbhu+vaḥ om uttarā parasyāṃ diśi vāyave namaḥ| oṃ bhū + vaḥ| teṃ vayagu…
sāma + aśimahi| tem oṃ bhū + (l3.) vaḥ uttarasyāṃ diśi kuberāya namaḥ| oṃ bhū + vaḥ| ruṃ tamīśānaṃ 
jagatastasthuṣaspatiṃ dhiyaṃjinvamavase bhūmahe vayam| pūṣā yathā vedasāmasaddhṛdherakṣitāpāyuradabdhasvastaye| ru bhū + vaḥ| om 
uttarapūrvasyāṃ diśi(l4.) īśānāya namaḥ| oṃ bhū + vaḥ| drām asme rudrā me bhanāparvatāso vṛtrahatye puruhūto sajoṣāḥ| yaḥ 
śaguṃsatestuvatedhāyi vajri indra jyeṣṭhā asmā avantu devāḥ| drām oṃ bhū + vaḥ| om ākāśāya namaḥ| (l5.) oṃ bhū + 
vaḥ| yam| syonāpṛthivī vivānṛkṣarā niveśani| yacchānaḥ śastha sa pṛthāḥ| yam oṃ bhū + vaḥ| oṃ pṛthivyai namaḥ| 
evamevātmani| 
lalāṭe netrakarṇau ca mukhabāhau ca nāsike| (p29b., f27a., sideheading ātmasaṃpuṭīkaraṇam, raudrīkaraṇam|, l1.)
hṛdaye nābhimūrdhni syāt pādayordaśa dehakam||

oṃ bhū + vaḥ| trātā + tvindraḥ| om oṃ bhūrbhuva + vaḥ| lalāṭe indrāya namaḥ| om oṃ bhū + vaḥ naṃ tvagno + smat| naṃ

yo'sau sarveṣu vedeṣu paṭhyatestaṃ raṃśca raḥ| akāyo nirguṇo hyātmā ta + stu||22||

māno mahānta + rīriṣastanme + stu||24||
mānastoke tanaye + vidhemate tanme + stu||25||
caturo vedānadhīyeta sarvaśāstramayaṃ viduḥ| itihāsapurāṇānāṃ na + satu||26||
kailāsaśikhare ramye śaṅkarasya *śivālaye| devatāstatra kṣedante tanme + stu||27||* (l5.)
yo nidre agnau + namo astu tanme + stu||28||
triyambakaṃ yajāmahe + tāt tanma + stu||29||
ṛtaṃ satyaṃ + virūpākṣaṃ ta + stu ||30||
kadrudrāya pracetase + namo astuta + stu||31||(p32a., f29b., l1.)
viśvataṃ cakṣu + ekasta + stu||32||
brahmajajñānaṃ prathamaṃ + vivasta + stu||33||
yaḥ prāṇato + vedhe mata + stu||34||
ya ātmadā + vidhema tanme + stu||35||
gandhadvārā + śriyaṃ ta + stu||36||
ya idaṃ śivasaṅkalpaṃ sadā dhyāyanti brāhmaṇāḥ| te parā mokṣaṃ gamiṣyāmi (l2.)tanme manaḥ śivasaṅkalpamastu||37||
hṛdaye śivāya namaḥ| yenedaṃ yena karmāṇi yena karmāṇyapaso yat prajānaṃ suṣāradhryasmin tṛco yadatra ṣaṣṭhaṃ yajjāgrato yenedaṃ 
viśvaṃ yena dyauḥ pṛthivī ye mano hṛdayamacintyaṃ caikācaye'paṃ(l3.) ca vedāhametaṃ yasyedaṃ dhīrāḥ paraṃ …..cai…parātparataraṃ 
brahmayā vedādiṣu yo vai devaṃ prayataḥ praṇavoṅkāraṃ yo'sau sarveṣu gobhirju…māno mahāntaṃ mānastoke caturo vedān (l4.) 
kailāsaśikhare yo rudro agnau triyambakaṃ ṛtaṃ satyaṃ kadrudrāya pracetase viśvata(ca?)kṣu brahmajajñānaṃ yaḥ prāṇato…ātmadā 
gandhadvārāṃ ya idaṃ śivasaṅkalpaṃ saptatriṃśat||

puruṣasūktaṃ śiraḥ|| 
sahasraśīrṣā(l5.)puruṣaḥ + sādhyāḥ santi devāḥ| śirasi puruṣāya namaḥ| uttaranārāyaṇaṃ śikhā| sahasraśīrṣaṃ deva …….masvarām| 
śikhāyāṃ nārāyaṇāya namaḥ| apratirathaṃ kavacaṃ āśuḥ śiśānaṣaśarmayacchatu| indrāya (p32b., f30a., l1.)kavacāya namaḥ| 
bibhrāṭ pratipuruṣadvayaṃ netram| pratipuruṣamekakapālāgni nṛpati + pinākā hastaḥ kṛttivāsāḥ| bibhrāṭ pratipuruṣaṃ pratipuruṣaṃ 
netratrayāyai vauṣaṭ| tvamagne rudra iti śatarudriyamityastram| tvamagne rudro + bhūrbhuvassuvaḥ| rudrāya (l2.) astrāya namaḥ| evaṃ 
pañcāṅgaṃ sakṛjjapet| 

urasā śirasā dṛṣṭyā manasā śraddhayāpi vā padbhyāṃ karābhyāṃ kaṇṭhābhāyaṃ praṇāmo'ṣṭāṅga ucyate|

hiraṇyagarbho yaḥ prāṇato brahmajajñānaṃ mahi dyaurupaśvāsayāgnenaya (l3.) yāte agne imaṃ yamaprastaramiti| hiraṇyagarbha + 
vidhemeti urasā rudrāya namaḥ| evaṃ sarvatra| yaḥ prāṇato + vidhemati| śirasā + brahmajajñānaṃ + vibhaḥ| dṛṣṭyā| mahidyauḥ + 
mabhiḥ| manasā| upaśvāsaya + śatran iti (l4.) vacasā agnenaya + vidhema iti| padbhyām| yāte agne …rūṇi| iti 
karābhyām| imaṃ yama + dādayasva iti karṇābhyām| ityaṣṭāṃ praṇamyā yātmānaṃ śrīrudrarūpaṃ dhyāyet -
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ triṇetraṃ pañcavaktrakam| (l5.) daśabhujaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam| nīlaśrīvaṃśabhinnaṃ nāgayajñopavītinam| 
nāgābharaṇabhūṣitam| vyāghracarmottarīyakam|
kamaṇḍalvakṣasūtradharaṃ abhayavaradapradam| śūlahastajvalantam| kapilajaṭinam| śikhāmudyotadhāriṇam|(p33a., f30b., l1.) 
vṛṣaskandhasamārūḍhamumādehārdhadhāriṇam|
amṛtenāplutaṃ hṛṣṭaṃ divyadehasamanvitam| digdevatā samāyūktaṃ surāsuranamaskṛtam||
nityaṃ ca śāśvataṃ śuddhaṃ dhṛ(dhru)vamakṣaramavyayam| sarvavyāpinamīśānaṃ rudraṃ vai viśvarūpiṇam||
evaṃ rūpiṇamevaṃ dhyātvā| (l2.) dvijaḥ samyaktato devayajanamārabhet| asya śrī rudrapraśnamahāmantrasya| aghorṛṣiḥ| 
anuṣṭupchandaḥ| saṅkarṣaṇamūrtisvarūpo yo'sāvādityaḥ paramapuruṣaḥ sa eṣa rudro devatā| namaśśivāyeti bījam| 
(l3.)śivatarāyeti śaktiḥ| sadāśivāyeti kīlagam| mama bhoga…..rthasidhyarthe dvārā śrī sāmbasadāśiva prītyarthe śivābhiṣeke 
viniyogaḥ| agnihotrātmane aṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ| darśapūrṇamāsātmane tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ| cāturmāsyātmane (l4.) madhyamābhyāṃ 
namaḥ| nirūḍhapaśubandhātmane anāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ| agniṣṭomātmane kaniṣṭikābhyāṃ namaḥ| catvāriṃśacchatasahasra kṛtvātmane 
karatalakarapṛṣṭābhyāṃ namaḥ| evamaṅganyāsaḥ| bhūrbhuvassuvaromiti digbandhaḥ| dhyānam - (l5.)
āpātālanabhasthalānnabhuvanabrahmāṇḍa(ṇḍā)tmā visphurat|
jyotisphaṭikaliṅgamaulivilasat pūrṇenduvāntāmṛtaiḥ||

dhyāyedīpsitasiddhaye drutapadaṃ *viprobhi'ṣiñcecchivam||* (p33b., f31a., l1.)

athāto rudrasnānārnābhiṣekavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ| ādidevatīrthe snātvā| udetvātmani …tā snāpayet| prajanane brahmā tiṣṭhatu| 
pādayorviṣṇustiṣṭhatu| hastayorharistiṣṭhatu| bāhvorindrastiṣṭhatu| jaṭhare agnistiṣṭhatu| (l2.) hṛdaye …..stiṣṭhatu| kaṇṭhe 
vasavastiṣṭhatu| vaktre sarasvatī tiṣṭhatu| nāsikayorvāyustiṣṭhatu| nayanayoścandrādityau tiṣṭhetām|  tya śuciḥ prayato 
brahmacāri śuklavāsasā sāsyadakṣiṇā pratyagdeśe tanmukhāsthi| lalāṭe rudrāstiṣṭhantu| mūrdhni ādityāstiṣṭhantu| śirasi 
mahādevastiṣṭhatu| (l3.) śikhāyāṃ vāmadevastiṣṭhatu| pṛṣṭhe pinākī tiṣṭhatu| purataḥ śūlī tiṣṭhatu| pārśvayoḥ śivāṃśaṃ karau 
tiṣṭhetām| sarvato vāyu tiṣṭhantu| tato bahiḥ sarvatognijvālāmālāparivṛtāḥ sarveṣvaṅgeṣu sarvā (l4.)devatā yathāsthānaṃ 
tiṣṭhantu| māṃ rakṣantu| agnirme vāci śruta iti yathā liṅgamaṅgāni sammṛjya| agnirme vāci śrutaḥ| brahmaṇi| iti vāci 
sammṛśya| vāyurme + brahmaṇi| iti pārṇe| sūryo me cakṣuṣi + brahmaṇi| iti cakṣuṣoḥ| candramā (l5.) me + brahmaṇi| iti 
manasi| diśo me śrotre + ṇi| iti śrotrayoḥ| āpo me + ṇi| iti retasi| pṛthivī me + ṇi| iti śarīre| 
oṣadhivanaspatayo me + ṇi| iti lomasu| (p34a., f31b., l1.) indro me bala + ṇi| iti bāla? parjanyo me + 
ṇi| iti mūrdhni| īśāno me manyau + ṇi| iti manyau| ātmā ma ātmani + ṇi| ityātmani| punarma ātmā + gopā 
iti| sarvāṅgeṣu sammṛśyātha vyāhṛtibhirnirmālyaṃ visṛjya| 
uttaraṃ caṇḍeśvarā(l2.)ya namaḥ iti| ithādbhistarpayāti| bhavaṃ devaṃ tarpayāmi| śarvaṃ devaṃ tarpayāmi| īśānaṃ devaṃ tarpayāmi| īśānaṃ 
devaṃ tarpayāmi| paśupatiṃ devaṃ tarpayāmi| rudraṃ devaṃ tarpayāmi| ugraṃ devaṃ tarpayāmi| bhīmaṃ devaṃ tarpayāmi| mahāntaṃ devaṃ tarpayāmi| 
iti tarpaya nama iti| athādbhistarpayā(ya)ti| bhavaṃ devaṃ tarpayāmi| śarvaṃ devaṃ tarpayāmi| īśānaṃ devaṃ tarpayāmi| paśupatiṃ devaṃ 
tarpayāmi| rudraṃ devaṃ tarpayāmi| ugraṃ devaṃ tarpayāmi| bhīmaṃ devaṃ tarpayāmi| mahāntaṃ devaṃ tarpayāmi| iti (l3.)tarpayitvāthainaṃ 
sākṣatapuṣpadhūpadīpanaivedyatāmbūlapradakṣiṇanamaskārairabhyarcāthātmānaṃ pratyārādhayet| ārādhito manuṣye'stu| siddhairdevāsurādibhiḥ| 
ārādhayāmi bhaktyā tvā| māṃ (l4.) grahāṇa maheśvara| iti prārthya| triyambakaṃ + tāt| iti dhyātvāthainamāvāhayet| ātvā 
vahantu harayaḥ sacetasaśvetairaśvaiḥ saha ketumadbhiḥ| vātāja vai balavadbhirmanojavairāyāhi śīghraṃ mama havyāya (l5.) śarvom| 
itīśānamāvāhayāmi| sadyojātaṃ prapadyāmītyāsanaṃ dadāti| sadyojātāya vai nama iti pādyam| bhave bhave nātibhave bhajasva 
māmityarghyam| bhavodbhavāya nama ityācamanīyam| vāmadevāya(p34b., f32a., l1.) nama ityabdhi(bdhya)ṅgābhiśca 
snānam| jyeṣṭhāya nama iti vastram| rudrāya nama ityupavītam| kālāya nama iti gandham| kalavikaraṇāya nama ityakṣatān| 
balavikaraṇāya nama iti puṣpāṇi| balapramathanāya nama iti dhūpam| sarvabhūtadamanāya nama iti dīpam| manonmanāya nama 
(l2.) iti dīpam| kāle naivedyam| athāsyāṣṭabhirmantrairaṣṭau puṣpāṇi dadāti| oṃ bhavāya devāya namaḥ + mahato devāya 
namaḥ| ityathāsyāghoratanurūpatiṣṭhate| aghorebhyastatpuruṣāyeti rudragāyatriṃ japet daśa (l3.) kṛtvā śataṃ kṛtvā sahasra(sraṃ) 
kṛtvopari te kṛtvothai māśiṣamāśāste| atha tanmūrdhni hiraṇyakalaśe nu santatamudakadhārānnyabhiṣa(ṣi)ñcan| payasā dadhnā 
sarpiṣā madhunāmrarasenekṣurasena nālikera(l4.)rasena gandhodakena vā| tadabhāve svādūdakena vā| śaṃ ca ma ityanuvākamādau namaste 
rudra manyava ityekādaśānuvākānām agnanā viṣṇū sajoṣasetyekādaśānuvākānāmekamekamanuvākaṃ japet| (l5.) 
sarveṣāmanne(gne) punarārādhayeduttarārādhanaṃ pāpakṣayārthavyādhināśanārthi śrīkāmaḥ śāntikāmaḥ puṣṭikāmastuṣṭikāma-ārogyakāmaḥ 
putrakāmo'nnādyakāmo yajñakāmo mokṣārthī ca kuryāt devakurvan| (p35a., f32b., sideheading 
aṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsam|, l1.) siddhimāpnotyācāryaḥ ….. savatsāṃ sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitāṃ dadyāttadabhāve yathāśakti dakṣiṇāṃ 
dadyādityāha bhagavān bodhāyanaḥ||

atha pañcabrahmanyāsaṃ kuryāt|
īśānassarvavidyānāṃ + śivom| (l2.) śirasi īśānāya namaḥ| tatpuruṣāya + dayāditi mukhe tatpuruṣāya namaḥ| aghorebhyo 
+ …bhya iti hṛdaye aghorāya namaḥ| vāmadevāya + nama iti guhye vāmadevāya namaḥ| sadyojātaṃ + nama iti pādayoḥ 
sadyojātāya namaḥ| (l3.) ityaṅguṣṭhādi kaniṣṭhāntāṅgulibhiḥ krameṇa vinyaset||

||athāṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsaṃ kuryāt||
1.	oṃ hoṃ īśānaḥ sarvavidyānām| śaśinyai namaḥ| ūrdhvamūrdhni|
2.	oṃ hoṃ īśvarassarvabhūtānām aṅgadāyai namaḥ| pūrvamūrdhni| (l4.)
3.	oṃ hoṃ brahmādhipatirbrahmaṇo'dhipatirbrahmeṣṭāyai namaḥ| dakṣiṇamūrdhni|
4.	oṃ hoṃ śivo me astu marīcyai namaḥ| uttaramūrdhni|
5.	oṃ hoṃ sadāśivoṃ jvālinyai namaḥ| paścimamūrdhni|
6.	oṃ hem avyaktakalāyai namaḥ| *ūrdhvavaktre|* (l5.)
7.	oṃ heṃ tatpuruṣāya vidmahe śāntyai namaḥ| pūrvavaktre|
8.	oṃ heṃ mahādevāya dhīmahi vidyāyai namaḥ| dakṣiṇavaktre|
9.	oṃ heṃ tanno rudrapratiṣṭhāyai namaḥ| uttaravaktre|
10.	oṃ heṃ pracodayāt *nivṛtyai namaḥ| paścimavaktre|* (p35b., f33a., l1.)  
11.	oṃ hum aghorebhyastamāyai namaḥ| hṛdaye| 
12.	oṃ hum athaghorebhyo mohāyai namaḥ| kaṇṭhe| 
13.	oṃ huṃ ghorarakṣāyai namaḥ| dakṣiṇāṃse| 
14.	oṃ huṃ aghorebhya niṣṭhāyai namaḥ| vāmāṃse|
15.	oṃ huṃ sarvataḥ *śarvamṛtyave namaḥ| nābhau|* (l2.)
16.	oṃ huṃ sarvebhyo māyāyai namaḥ| jaṭhare|
17.	oṃ huṃ namaste astu rudrabhayāyai namaḥ| pṛṣṭhe|
18.	oṃ huṃ rūpebhyo jarāyai namaḥ| urasi|
19.	oṃ hiṃ vāmadevāya namo rajāyai namaḥ| guhye|
20.	oṃ hiṃ jyeṣṭhāyai *namo rakṣāyai namaḥ| liṅge|* (l3.)
21.	oṃ hiṃ rudrāya namo ratyai namaḥ| dakṣiṇorau|
22.	oṃ hiṃ kalāyai namaḥ| pālyai namaḥ| vāmorau|
23.	oṃ hiṃ kalakāmāyai namaḥ| dakṣiṇajānunī|
24.	oṃ hiṃ vikaraṇāyai namaḥ| saṃyamanyai namaḥ| *vāmajānunī|* (l4.)
25.	oṃ hiṃ balakriyāyai namaḥ| dakṣiṇajaṅghāyām|
26.	oṃ hiṃ vikaraṇāya namaḥ| budyai(dhyai) namaḥ| vāmajaṅghāyām|
27.	oṃ hiṃ balakāryai namaḥ| dakṣiṇasphici|
28.	oṃ hiṃ pramathanāya namo dhātryai namaḥ| vāmasphici|
29.	oṃ hiṃ sarvabhūta*damanāya namo bhrāmiṇyai namaḥ| kaṭyām|* (l5.)
30.	oṃ hiṃ manonmanyai namaḥ| dakṣiṇapārśve|
31.	(oṃ hiṃ) unmanāya namo bhavāyai namaḥ| vāmapārśve|
32.	oṃ haṃ sadyojātaṃ prapadyāmi siddhyai namaḥ| dakṣiṇapāde|
33.	oṃ haṃ sadyojātāya vai namaḥ ṛdhyai namaḥ| *vāmapāde|*(p36a., f33b., l1.)
34.	oṃ haṃ …….(bhavedyutyai namaḥ| dakṣiṇa)pāṇau|
35.	oṃ haṃ anādibhave namaḥ| medhāyai namaḥ| nāsāyām|
36.	oṃ haṃ bhajasva māṃ kāntyai namaḥ| śirasi|
37.	oṃ haṃ bhavasvadhāyai namaḥ| dakṣiṇabāhau|
38.	oṃ haṃ *udbhavāya namo dhṛtyai namaḥ| vāmabāhau|* (l2.)

asya śrī pañcākṣaranyāsamahāmantrasya| vāmadeva ṛṣiḥ| paṅktiśchandaḥ| sāmbasadāśivo devatā| oṃ bījam| namaśśaktiḥ| 
śivāya kīlakam| mama śarīranyāse viniyogaḥ| 

||atha pañcākṣaranyāsaḥ||(l3.)
(oṃ namaḥ) śikhāyām| oṃ namaḥ| śirasi naṃ namaḥ| mukhe maṃ namaḥ|  hṛdaye śiṃ namaḥ| guhye vaṃ namaḥ| pādayoḥ yaṃ namaḥ| 

atha śrīkaṇṭhamantra(mātṛ)kānyāsaḥ| asya śrī śrīkaṇṭhamātṛkānyāsamahāmantrasya dakṣiṇāmūrti ṛṣiḥ| gāyatrīcchandaḥ| 
(l4.)akṣarātmikā sarasvatī devatā| haṃ bījam| svaḥ śaktiḥ| mama mokṣārthe śarīranyāse viniyogaḥ| oṃ hrīṃ śrīm aṃ

sūkṣmaśālibhyāṃ namaḥ| dakṣiṇanetre (iti krameṇa)| bījākṣaraṃ sarvatra īṃ trimūrtilolākṣi्bhayāṃ namaḥ| vāmanetre| um 
amareśvaravartulābhyāṃ namaḥ| dakṣiṇakarṇe| ūm arpiśadīrghakoṇābhyāṃ namaḥ| (p36b., f34a., l1.) vāmakarṇe| ṛṃ 
bhārabhūtidīrghamukhī…..dakṣiṇa….puṭe| ṛṃ nidhaneśagomukhībhyāṃ namaḥ| vāmanāsāpuṭe| ḷṃ sthāṇuve dīrghajihvābhyāṃ namaḥ| 
dakṣiṇagaṇḍe| ḹṃ harakuṇḍodarībhyāṃ vāmagaṇṭhe(kaṇṭhe/gaṇḍe)| eṃ …… rdhvakeśibhyāṃ namaḥ| (l2.) uttaroṣṭhe| aiṃ bhautikā–kṛta—
bhyoṃ adhare — oṃ sadyojātajvālāmukhībhyāṃ uttaradantapaṅktau| aum anugraheśolkāmukhibhyām adharadantapaṅktau| am 
akrūraśrīmukhibhyāṃ mūrdhni| (l3.) mahāsenavidyābhyām āsye| kaṃ? krodheśamahākālībhyāṃ dakṣabhuje| khaṃ caṇḍeśasarasvatībhyāṃ 
dakṣakorpare| gaṃ pañcāntakasarvasiddhibhyāṃ dakṣamaṇibandhe| ghaṃ śivottamatrailokyavidyābhyāṃ dakṣahaste| (l4.) ṅam 
ekarudramantraśaktibhyāṃ dakṣāṅguliṣu| caṃ kuskeśātmaśaktibhyāṃ vāmabhuje| chaṃ ekanetrabhūtamātṛbhyāṃ vāmakorpare| jaṃ 
caturmukhalambādaribhyāṃ vāmamaṇibandhe| jham ajeśabrāmaṇibhyāṃ vāma…..(l5.) ñaṃ candragatakari……māguliṣu| ṭaṃ

ardhanārīśavikariṇībhyāṃ dakṣapāde| ṇam umākānta…(p37a., f34b., l1.) sāṃ dakṣapādāṅguliṣu| tam āṣāḍha…pūtanābhyāṃ

prārthanā -

agnaya idam| prajāpataye svāhā| prajāpataya idam| (l4.) śeṣaṃ pūrvavadācaret| brahmacārī ca prātarvat| vidhurastu pradhānāhutyāḥ 
gṛhasthavat kuryāt| pūrvavadvaiśvadevaṃ baliharaṇaṃ ca kṛtvā| śvāna-vāyasabaliṃ (l5.) na kuryāt| kecittu vaiśvadehahomaṃ kṛtvā|

ucchiṣṭadeśaṃ (p50a., f47b., l1.) bhasmanopalipya| prokṣayet| kecicchiṣṭa….| adhyāye pradoṣeṣu ca sāyaṃ 
gāyatrijapasaṃkocamācaranti| āhuśca| nityādardhaṃ caturthi-vasu-manu-tithiṣu syājjapaḥ saptamī yā saptatriṃśacca tasyāṃ 
prati(l2.)padiva punaḥ ṣaḍyuta triṃśadevam| tadvat parvadvaye'pi triyutadaśatithāvasminviśati syādevaṃ 
viṣvādikāleṣvakhilamatamidaṃ sāyamevaṃ na kālye| iti tathā vā kartavyaṃ bhojanāntaraṃ tāmbūlacaraṇaṃ(carvaṇaṃ) ca (l3.) kṛtvā|

ricaryā bhaikṣāhārādhaḥ śayana-pradoṣa-paścimayāmā(p51a., f48b., sideheading snātakadharmaḥ|, l1.)dhyāyanamiti 
brahmacāryastu niyamataḥ kuryāt| tatra niyamaḥ| ekayajñopavītī| kaupīnakṛṣṇājinamuttarīya-bhasma-rudrākṣadharo mauñjī 
yatpalāśadaṇḍadhṛkkamaṇḍaludharaḥ| khaṇḍapāsaḥ paridhānaḥ| pavitradhṛgācāramātrabhai(l2.)kṣācaraṇaḥ| ajñātādhikabhaikṣe 
adhikamanuśiṣṭameva bhūmau vā nikhanedapsu vā praveśayet| pratidinaṃ samidāharaṇam| samidādhānaṃ ca kuryāt| tāmbūla-maithuna-
madhu-māṃsasevana-sūtikādīni varjayan| guruniyuktaṃ sarvamācaret| evamāsamāvartanāt| bodhāyanamaḥ|
||atha snātakadharmaḥ||
atha snātakasyāntarvāsyuttarīyaṃ vaiṇavadaṇḍaṃ dhārayet|
sodakaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum|
||dviyajñopavītī||(l4.)
uṣṇīṣamajinamuttarīyopānahau chatraṃ caupāsanaṃ darśapūrṇamāsau varvasu ca| keśaśmaśrulomanakhavāpanaṃ tasya vṛtti-brāhmaṇa-rājanya-
vaiśya-rathakāreṣāmaulispeta(l5.)bhaikṣaṃ vā vāgyatasviṣṭet| sarvāṇi cāsya devapitṛsaṃyuktāni pākayajñikasaṃsthāni bhūtikarmāṇi 
kurvītaina vidhinā prajāpateḥ paraneṣṭhinaḥ| paramarṣayaḥ paramāṃ (p51b., f49a., sideheading gṛhasthadharmaḥ, l1.) kāṣṭhāṃ 
gacchanti| samāvartanānantaram| daivādvivāhavighne jāte| punarāvivāhāt| devamanuṣṭheyam| yāvajjīvaṃ vā aupāsane tu sāyaṃ 
prātarājyena vyāhṛtibhirjuhuyāt| anyatsarvaṃ samānam| (l2.)
|| gṛhasthadharmaḥ ||
dakṣaḥ| 
sandhyāsnānaṃ japahomasvādhyāyaṃ devatārcanam|
vaiśvadevekṣaṇātithyamudakaṃ pāsva śaktitaḥ||
kuryācchuddhena manasā satkarmāṇi sadā dvijaḥ|
vai śūnyamanṛtaṃ *māyāṃ kāmakrodhaṃ tathā priyam||* (l3.)
dveṣaṃ saṅgaṃ paradrohaṃ vikarmaṇi vivarjayet|
nṛttagītaṃ kṛṣiḥ sopā vāṇijyaṃ lavaṇaṃ kriyaḥ||
bhūtakarmā *śraddhā prajñā tyāgaḥ kṛtajñatā|* (l4.)
ete yasya guṇāḥ santi gṛhastho mukhya ucyate||
śrutismṛtyuditaṃ dharmamanutiṣṭhanhi mānavaḥ|
iha kīrtimavāpnoti pretya cānṛtta māṃ gatiḥ||
varṇāśramavidhaṃ kṛtsnaṃ *kurvāṇo tat parāyaṇaḥ|* (l5.) 
tenaiva janmanā jñānaṃ labdhvā yāti śivaṃ padam||
yajanaṃ yājanaṃ caiva adhyayanādhyāpakaṃ tathā|
dānaṃ pratigrahaṃ caiva ṣaṭkarmāṇi dine dine|| bodhāyanaḥ ||(p52a., f49b., l1.)
nityodaki nityayajñopavītī nityasvādhyāyī vṛṣalānnavarjī| ṛtau gacchanvidhivacca juhvan| na brāhmaṇaścuvate lokāt| 
āyuṣā tapasā yuktasvādhyāyo jyā parāyaṇaḥ| 
prajāmutpādayedyuktā sve sve varṇajite*ndriyaḥ||* (l2.)
svādhyāyena ṛṣīn pūjyā(ya) somena ca purandaram|
prajayā ca pitṝn pūrvānanṛṇodivi modate||
putreṇa lokā jayati pautreṇānandamaśnute|
atha putrasya pautreṇa nākamevādhirohayet|| (l3.)
saptāparān saptapūrvan ṣaḍanyāsānātmasaptamān|
satputramadhigacchānāstārayatyenaso bhavet||
mātāpitro hi te yukte gobrāhmaṇahite rataḥ|
yajvā ca devabhaktaśca brahmaloke *mahīyate||*(l4.)
trivargasevi satatadevatānāṃ ca pūjanam|
kuryādaharahaṃ nityaṃ namasyet satataṃ surāt||
vibhāgaśīlasatataṃ kṣamāyukto dayāluka|
gṛhasthastu samākhyāto na gṛheṇa gṛhī *bhavet||* (l5.)
athāśakti caret karmaninditāni ca varjayet|
vidhūya mohakalalaṃ labdhvā yogamanuttamam||
gṛhastho mucyate bandhānnātra kāryavicāraṇa|
nivāpno pitṛnarcannyojñairdevāṃ *tathātithīn||*(p52b., f50a., l1.)
antairmuniśca svādhyāyairapatyena prajāpatim|
balikṛtyena bhūtāni vātsalyo nākhilaṃ jagat||
prāpnoti lokān puruṣo nijakarmasamājitān|
yathā nadinadāḥ sarve sāgare yāni saṃsthitim|| (l2.)
tathaivā…miṇaḥ sarve gṛhastho yānti saṃsthitam|
sarveṣāmeva caiteṣāṃ vedasmṛtividhānataḥ||
gṛhastha ucyate śreṣṭhāḥ sa trinetan bibhakti hi|
kaṭisūtraṃ vinā karmāśrautasmārtaṃ karoti yaḥ||
sarvaṃ na niṣphalaṃ vidyāt *so'pi nagna iti śrutiḥ|* (l3.)
karṇe puṣpaṃ kaṭau sūtraṃ vastreṇa pariveṣṭitam||
dhārayedyadi mūḍhātmā sarvakarmabahiṣkṛtaḥ|
baddhacūḍo'pi piṭharaṃ śikhaspṛkkarma yaścaret||
tatkarma niṣphalaṃ jñeyaṃ *bahirjānū ca yat kṛtam|* (l4.)
karmakāle ca nābhiśca sandhijānudvayaṃ tathā||
vastreṇācchādya matimān karma kuryādatandritaḥ|
kacchapucchadharo viprā upavītottarīyakaḥ||
nityakarmāṇi kurvīta prayataḥ śuddhamānasaḥ|(l5.)
yajñopavītavaddhāryamuttarīyaṃ dvijātinā||
vandane tarpaṇe home snāne kaṭyāṃ tu dhārayet|
ṣaṇḍapāṣaṇḍapatitamlecchādibhirna saṃbhāṣeta||
tānapi na vikṣeta yadi vikṣetādityamavalokayet| (p53a., f50b., l1.)
anyastrībhiḥ pure …. bhāṣeta yadi saṃbhāṣeta tāvadvivikte…vadvācyaṃ saṃbhāṣeta| caṇḍālāmbaṣṭharajakādibhi(bhiḥ) rātrau na 
saṃbhāṣeta tadvākyaṃ ca na śruṇuyāt| vṛthā śūdrairna saṃbhāṣeta| yadi saṃbhāṣeta nivītī saṃbhāṣeta| (l2.) etadasacchūdraviṣayam| 
bahiḥ sañcaritvā pādau prakṣālya gṛhaṃ praviśet| 
śūdragṛhaṃ praviśyā(śya) puna(naḥ) snātvā svagṛhaṃ praviśet| na śūdrāya dharmamupadiśet| na mantraṃ ca nāstikādibhyaśca| 
śiṣṭadānapratigraha(l3.)saṃbhāṣaṇādi kāryam| taiḥ sahaḥ kālakṣepakāryaḥ| divā svāpaṃ svabhāryāgamanaṃ ca varjayet| nāprayato vaset| 
bheryādi tilayantraṃ cakrayātrādyārāvaṃ śruṇvanna bhujyāt| (l4.) na vṛkṣāgramārohenna parvatamārohayet| bāhubhyāṃ na nadiṃ taret| na 
viṣādinā dehaṃ pātayet| sarvadhātmānaṃ gopāyet| divā ca śirasi prāvaraṇaṃ varjayenmūtrapurīṣayoḥ (l5.) karma parisamāpya| nāpsu 
mūtrapurīṣaniṣṭhipanāni kuryāt nāpsu guhyaṃ darśayan sañcaret| na nagnaḥ kṣaṇamātramapi vaset| phalādīni na nakhairnikṛntayet| 
nagnaṃ nagnaṃ (p53b., f51a., l1.) saṃsaktamaithunaṃ vā na paśyet yadi paśyet sūryaṃ paśyet| nāpnoṭayenna kṣelayet| na 
nṛtyaṃ na goyet| nakharomāṇi na chidyāt| na loṣṭaṃ bhindyānna tṛṇaṃ chindyāt| nākṣeḥ krīḍanna viṇmūtra(l2.)mudikṣeta 
pādena pāṇinā vā jalaṃ nāspā(phā)layet| pādena pādaṃ na prakṣālayet| sandhyāyorna svet| na gacchenna gṛhaṃ(ha) kṛtyaṃ 
kuryānna bhujyāt| budhyā kṛmikīṭādiṃ na hanyāt| (l3.) śiṣṭai pitrādibhiśca kalahaṃ na kuryāt| kṣute  śatajiveti skhalite 
pāṃsunādāya lalāṭe nidhāya| jṛmbhaṇe aṅgulisphoṭanena parihartavyam| nityanaimittikaṃ caiva (l4.) karma kurvanna tandritaḥ| 
niṣiddhaṃ varjayandhīmānnihāmutra sukhaṃ bhavet| 
|| atha tailābhyaṅgavidhiḥ ||
indo budhaśca saureśca vāre abhyaṅga iṣyate| 
dvitīyādyāścatasraśca *praśastā saptamī tathā|*(l5.)
navati daśamī caiva trayodaśyapi ca smṛtā||
uttarā phalgunīṃ tyaiṣā śravaṇārdrāsu rātriṣu|
abhyaṅgasnapanaṃ dhīmān sukhārthī varjayettathā||
niṣiddhadine'pi śanivāre *na doṣaḥ idamabhyañjanam|* (p54a., f51b., l1.) prātaḥkālasāyaṃkālayorvarjyam| 
doṣasmaraṇāt| bālavṛddhāturāṇāṃ na niyamaḥ| dravyānnarasaṃ sṛṣṭatailasyāpi na doṣaḥ| parahastena tailābhyaṅgamardanaṃ kārayet| 
(l2.) prāṅmukhadakṣiṇāmukhaḥ| pratyaṅmukhā ca udaṅmukhastu na kuryāt| upaviśyābhyaṅgaṃ kārayitva(vā)vasāne kārī(yi)tuḥ| 
pāṇi samṛśati| balaṃ dhe(de)hi tanūṣuṇo balamindrā tanū tyajaḥ| balaṃ te kāyatanayāya(l3.) jīvase tvaṃ hi baladā asi| 
asmīti prativacanam| tasmācchūdrādinā na kārayet| hemānte ghaṭikā syāt śiśire ghaṭikātrayam| vasennāḍikāḥ pañca grīṣme 
ṣaṇṇāḍikāstathā| varṣā(l4.)kāle śaratkāle sadyasnānaṃ samācaret|
|| atha kṣaurakālaḥ ||
sarve māsā kṣaurakālaḥ| śuci nabhasya sahastapo varjyam| 
śukrajīvajñacandrāṇāṃ vārā kṣaure śubhāvahāḥ|| (l5.) 
arkārkibhūmiputrāṇāṃ vārā kṣaure vivarjitāḥ| 
pāpagrahāṇāṃ vārādau viprāṇāṃ tu śubhe rave||
kṣatriyāṇāṃ kṣamā sū(śū)ro viṭch्śūdrāṇāṃ śanaistatha(thā)|
somavāre siteśasta…*kṛṣṇapakṣe tu garhitaḥ||* (p54b., f52a., l1.)
akṣaure ca nakṣatre kurvīta budhasomayoḥ|
mūlānurādhābhyāṃ brahmāpyabdaṃ na jīvati||
dvitīyyā ca tṛtīyyā cā(ca) pañcamī saptamī tathā|
daśamyekādaśī caiva trayodaśyapi *pūjitāḥ||* (l2.)
dvādaśī madhyamā proktā śiṣṭastu parivarjayet|
citrā śraviṣṭhahastāśvipauṣṇādityāśca vaiṣṇavaḥ||
mṛgaśīrṣaṃ ca puṣyaṃ ca kṣurakarmaṇi pūjitāḥ|
svātī śatabhiṣaṃ ca *maitraṃ rohiṇītrayuttarāḥ||* (l3.)
madhyamā vapane proktā śiṣṭāni parivarjayet|
karkikanyātulāmīnā yamagomakarā(rāḥ) śubhāḥ||
śeṣastu varjayedrāśīn kṣaurakarmāṇi sarvadā|
śuddhi *kuryāttathā vidyānmālānādehajanmanām||* (l4)
kṛttakeśanakhaśmaśru strīpakṣeṣu gṛhī bhavet|
||kṣauravidhiḥ||
prātaḥkāle tu tannivartya nāpitamāhūya saṅga(śaṅkha)vādyavartanāvadhi| viviktasthale (l5.) prāṅmukha-udaṅmukho 
vopaviśyā(śya)| nivītī bhūya jalapātraspṛgāvasānaṃ nāpitamabhimukhaṃ niveśya prakoṣṭhaṃ triśiśāṃ varjayitvā| prathamaṃ 
dakṣiṇakarabāhumūlaṃ tato vāmakarabāhumūlaṃ (p55a., f52b., l1.) paścāt galādhaḥ prabhṛti nābhyantaṃ tataḥ| 
piṭharādipṛṣṭhabhāge kaṭiparyantam| tataśmaśrū śikhāmanunāsikāntasthaṃ ca| dakṣiṇādi tadanukeśān sarvān atha guhyapradeśaṃ tato 
dakṣiṇapādaṃ tato vāmapādam| (l2.) evaṃ vāpayet| tato nakhāni kṛntayet| sūtakādau niṣiddhadine kṣaurakarma kṛtaṃ cet| 
tadanantaramuktānukūladine punareva vāpayet| evaṃ kṛte āyu(yuḥ) vṛddhirbhavati| āyuṣya(l3.)karmatvadidameva vicārya kāryam| 
tato nadyādau gatvā snātvā punarvastramupahatya mṛttikāsnānaṃ kṛtva(tvā) tatastrayodaśanimajjanaṃ kṛtvā| snānatarpaṇādikaṃ 
kṛtvā| ārdravastraṃ parityajya śuṣkavastraṃ (l4.) paridhāya| punareva yathoktaṃ snāyāt| śuṣkavastrābhāve ārdravastraṃ niṣpīḍya 
saptadhā vidhūyātape darśitva(tvā) punardhṛtvā snāyāt| evaṃ kṛte karmārhako bhavati| (p55b., f53a., l1.)
|| atha strīdharmaḥ ||
bhartuḥ śarīraśuśrūṣā dharmakāryaṃ ca naityakam|
anukūlatayā kuryāt bharturiṣṭā sadā bhavet||
bhartaryaprabuddhe sati svayaṃ purabuddhya bahi(hiḥ) nirgatyā(tya) jalamalādi visarjanaṃ kṛtvā| patyupadiṣṭa(l2.)śaucaṃ kṛtvā| 
dantaśuddhiṃ kṛtvācamyā(mya) gṛhaṃ gomayatoyaiḥ saṃsicyā(cya) sammārjyā(rjya) nadyādau kaṇṭhasnānaṃ kṛtvā vastraṃ paridhāya| 
haridrādilepanaṃ kṛtvā| sūryaṃ namaskṛtya patyupadiṣṭamantraṃ japtvā| gṛhamāgatyā(tya) jalā(l3.)haraṇādikaṃ ca kṛtvā| 
agnihotre(trau)pāsane vā paricaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhāṇḍakṣālanādikaṃ ca vidhāya| bālādirakṣābhāṇḍasthitaṃ taṃ(nnaṃ-tamannaṃ) bālādibhyo 
datvā| gṛhaṃ alaṅkṛtyā(tya) anantaraṃ devatārthaṃ (l4.)savyañjanaṃ pacet| tato devatānivedana-vaiśvadevabaliharaṇā 
tithipūjanabhartṛbhojana-bālādibhojanāntaraṃ bhujyāt| bharturyadiṣṭaṃ bhuñjyāt| yadaniṣṭaṃ tat tyajet| sāyaṃkāle gṛhamalaṅkṛtya| 
(l5.) dīpaṃ prajvālyā(lya)| sāyamagnihotraupāsane vā vaiśvadevabaliharaṇādi tithipūjanabhartṛbhojane ca bālabhojane ca 
paricaraṇaṃ kṛtvā| svayamavaśiṣṭaṃ bhuñjyāt| bharturi supte svayaṃ supyāt| (p56a., f53b., l1.) yadā bhartāratotsuko 
bhavati| tadā tadanukūlayā bhāvyā bhartaridite maraditā ārte ārtā proṣite malināmbarā bhukte bhuktā upoṣṭite upoṣṭitā kruddhe 
bhītā| yat pāne tat pānāmṛte mriyate striyaḥ| patireva(l2.) devatam| upavāsavratādikaṃ na kuryāt| yadi kuryāt bhartṛrājñayā 
kuryāt| bhojanakāle jyeṣṭā(ṣṭhā)yai namaḥ| iti bhojya dannāt| kiñcit jyeṣṭā(ṣṭhā) baliṃ dadyāt| 
tailābhyaṅgaśarīrodvartana-dantadhāvanālake śodhana-vastradhāraṇā-nidrā(l3.)-bhojana-vamana-śarīrālaṅkaraṇādīni kāryāṇi bhartari 
paśyati sati na kuryāt| evaṃvidhānāni svargamavāpnoti|
|| atha vidhavādharmaḥ ||
patyau mṛte yā nārī vaidhavyaṃ pālayet kvacit|
sā *puna(naḥ) prāpya bhartāraṃ svargabhojān samaśnute||* (l4.)
…dhavākabaribandho bharturbandhāya jāyate|
śirasopapannaṃ tasmāt kāryaṃ vidhavayā tathā||
ekahāraḥ sadā kāryo na dvitīyyaḥ kadācanā(na)|
trirātraṃ pañcarātraṃ vā *pakṣavratamathāpi vā|* (l5.)
māsopamāsaṃ vā kuryāt cāndrāyaṇamathāpi vā||
kuryāt kṛcchraṃ parākaṃ vā taptakṛcchramathāpi vā|
yavā naivaphalāhāraiḥ śākahāraiḥ payo ghṛtaiḥ||
prāṇayātraṃ *prakurvīta yāvat pāraṇasva(ssva)yaṃ vrajet|* (p56b., f54a., l1.)
paryaṃ kṛtvā yā nārī vidhavā pātayet patim||
tasmāt bhūśayanaṃ kāryaṃ patisaukhyaṃ samīhayā|
na cāṅgodvartanaṃ kāryaṃ striyā vidhavayā kvacit||
tarpaṇaṃ pratyahaṃ kāryaṃ bhartuḥ kuśatilodakaiḥ| (l2.)
tatpituḥ tat pituścāpi nāmagotrābhipūrvakam||
devatāpūjanaṃ kāryaṃ patibuddhyā ca nānyathā|
patimeva sadā dhyāyeddevarūpadharaṃ param||
evaṃ caryā parāṃ nityā vidhavāpi śubhā matā|
japecca śūdravasnātu* na me dravimathavau||* (l3.)
dīpaṃ ca bhartṛcittābhyāṃ nānyathā ca vidhīyate|
japaṃ ca prāṇapastasyā vaidikaistu vidhīyyate||
matai bhartari yā nārī brahmacarye vyavasthitā|
sā mṛtā labhate svargaṃ yathā te brahmacāriṇaḥ||
yāvajjīvaṃ (l4.) pretapatyudakopasparśanamekabhuktamadha(dhaḥ)śayyābrahmacaryaṃ kṣaralavaṇamadhumāṃsavarjanaśceti| udite sūrye 
snānādikarma kāryam| tīrthayātrādau putrādibhiḥ saha gacchet| tai(taiḥ) snānādikarma kṛtvā| dīrthaśrāddha(l5.)dānahomādikaṃ taiḥ 
kārayet| yāvajjīvaṃ dharmaśravaṇaniratā nāmakīrtanaṃ ca kurvatībhi(bhiḥ) mṛyeta sā svargaṃ prāpya panarbhartāramadhigacchati| iti 
kāśyapakulatilakajñānaśivācāryavaṃśasaṃbhūtaśrīpañcākṣara-guru(p57a., f54b., l1.)nāthaviracitāyāṃ karmaprakāśikāyāṃ 
nityakarmaparicchedaḥ samāptaḥ| śrī gurubhyo namaḥ| (p57b., f55a., sideheading śubhamastu bodhāyanamantrapraśnam|, 
l1.) 
diśāpatiṃ namasyāmi sarvakāmaphalapradān|
kurvantu saphalaṃ karma kurvantu satataṃ śubham||
agnimīle purohitaṃ yajñasya deva mṛtvijam|
hotāraṃ ratnadhātamam|
hiraṇyavarṇāṃ hariṇīṃ suvarṇarajatasrajām|
candrāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ jāta*vedo mamāvaha||* (l2.)
tām āvaha jātavedo lakṣmī manapagāminīm|
yasyāṃ hiraṇyaṃ vindeyaṃ gāmaśvaṃ puruṣānaham||
aśvapūrvāṃ rathamadhyāṃ  hastinātha prabodhanīm|
śriyaṃ devīmupahvaye| śirmādevī juṣatāṃ (l3.)*kāṃsosmitāṃ hiraṇyaprākārāmādrāṃ jvantīṃ triptāṃ tarpayantīṃ padme sthitāṃ padmavarṇāṃ 
tvāmiho pakvaye śriyam| 
candrāṃ prabhāsāṃ yaśasā jvalantīṃ  śriyaṃ loke deva juṣṭāmudārām| (l4.) 
tāṃ padmanemiṃ śaraṇamahaṃ prapadye lakṣmīrme naśyatāṃ tvāṃ vṛṇe|| 
ādityavarṇe tapaso'dhijāto vanaspatistava vṛkṣotha bilvaḥ|
tasya phalāni tapasā nudantu māyāṃ nirāyāśca bāhyā *lakṣmīḥ||* (l5.)
upaiyitu māṃ deva saha kīrtiśca manunā saha|
prātarbhūto'smi rāṣṭresmin kīrtivṛddhiṃ tadātu me||
kṣutpipāsāmalāṃ jṣṭā(ṣṭhā)lakṣmīmaṃ nāśayāmham|
abhūtimasamṛddhiṃ ca sarvāṇyānuda me gṛhāt|| (p58a., f55b., l1.)
gandhadvārāṃ dhurādharṣāṃ nityapuṣṭiṃ karīṣiṇīm|
īśvarīṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ tvāmi hopahvaye śriyam||
manasaḥ kāmamākūtiṃ vācaḥ satyamaśīmahi|
paśūnāguṃ rūpamannasya madhiśri śriyatāṃ yaśaḥ| 
kardamena prabhā *bhūtāmadhisaṃbhava kardama||* (l2.)
śriyaṃ vāsaya me kule mātaraṃ padmamālinīm|
āpassṛjantu smigdhāni cikvīta vasave gṛhe||
nija devīṃ mātaraṃ śriyaṃ vāsaya me kule|
ārdrāṃ puṣkaraṇiṃ puṣṭiṃ suvarṇāṃ hemamālinīm||(l3.)
sūryāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ jātavedo mamā vaha|
ārdrāṃ yaṣkaraṇīṃ yaṣṭiṃ piṅgalāṃ padmamālinīm||
candrāṃ hiraṇmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ jātavedo mamāvaha|
tāṃ ma āvaha jātavedo lakṣmīmanapagāminīm|| (l4.)
yasyāṃ hiraṇyaṃ prabhūtaṃ gāvo dāśvośvāt viśandayaṃ puruṣānaham|
padmapriye padmani padmahaste padmālaye padmadalāyatākṣi| 
viśvapriye viṣṇumanonukūle tvatpādaṃ padmaṃ *dhīmayi sannidhasva||* (l5.)
mahādevī ca vidmahe viṣṇupatnī ca dhīmahi|
tanno lakṣmī pracodayāt| 
viśve trāte savaneṣu pravācyā yā ca katthā maghavannindra sunvate|
pārāvataṃ yat puru*saṃ(ṣaṃ) sṛdaṃ vasvapāvṛṇoḥ śarabhāya…..||* (p58b., f56a., l1.)
yo brahmā brahmaṇa ujjahāraḥ prāṇeśvaraḥ kṛttivāsāḥ pinākī|
īśāno devaḥ sa na āyurdadhāti tasmai juhomi haviṣā gṛtena||
bibhrājamānaḥ sarīrasamadhyādedrā ca māno *gharmirucirya āgāt|* (l2.)
sa mṛtyupāśā ra….rāni hāyuṣe ṇoghṛtamattu devaḥ|
brahmajyotirbrahmapat niṣu garbhaṃ yamādadhāt pururūpajñayantam||
suvarṇarambhagrahamarkamarcyaṃ tamāyuṣe vardhayāmo gṛtena|
śriyaṃ *lakṣmīmabalāmambikāṃ gāṃ ṣaṣṭhiṃ yajāmi …natyudāhuḥ||* (l3.)
tāṃ vidyāṃ brahmayoniṃ surūpāmihāyuṣe tarpayāmo ghṛtena|
dākṣāyaṇyaḥ sarvayonyaḥ suyonyaḥ sahasraśo viśvarūpā virūpāḥ||
sa sūnavaḥ *supatayaḥ suyotthā āyuṣeṇo …..daṃ juṣantām|* (l4.)
divyā gaṇā bahurūpāḥ purāṇā āyucchido naḥ pramandhantu vīrān||
tebhyo juhomi bahudhā ghṛtena mānaḥ prajāṃ ririṣo me ….virā…|
ekaḥ *purastādya idaṃ  babhūva yato babhūva bhuvanasya gopāḥ||* (l5.)
yamapyeti bhuvanaṃ sāṃ parā ye sa noha vighṛtamihāyuṣe tu devaḥ|

l1.)ścāṅgirasotha sarvān ghṛtaṃ hutvā svāyuṣyāmahayāmaśvat| viṣṇo tvanno antamaśśarmayaccha sa hantyā pratedhārāmadhuścata

citraḥ kṣatraṃ citratamaṃ va bodhāḥ candraṃ, rayi(viṃ), puruvīraṃ, bṛhantaṃ candracandrābhiḥ śruṇute yuvasva|| somaṃ rājānaṃ

bibādhithāḥ| niṝtyai tvā putra(l5.)māhuḥ sanaḥ karmāṇi sādhaya|| virūpākṣamahāyaje nijaṃghaguṃ śabalodaram| yomāyaṃ 
paribādhate śriyai puṣṭyai ca nityatā tasmai| yātiraścīnipadyase'haṃ vidharaṇi iti tvāṃ tvāghṛtasya dhārayāṃ nausaṃrādhani yaje||

prathamāśvāsaḥ||1||(l4.)
viṣṇuṃ yoniṃ kalpayatu tvaṣṭā rūpāṇi piṃśatu| āsīṃ catu prajāpatirdhātā garbha idhā kṛte| yathāgnigarbhā pṛthivī dyauryathendreṇa

(l2.) ahaṃ garbhamadadhāmoṣadhināmahaṃ paśveṣu bhuvaneṣvanta…| ahaṃ prajā ajanayan manināmahaṃ janibhyo apariṣu putrān| tvāṃ

strīṣu me nṛtam| ajāsumerkaśaḥ| vrātyoma ītyā| śūdro me steyam| vaiśye me kārmakṛtyam| rājanya bandhuni me jñānam| naiṣāde 
me brahmahatyā| kuliṅge me kṣavadhūru(l4.) lale devilāsa| udīrṇameva- maniḥ| kiṃ puruṣe me rodo| dvipani me niṣṭapat|

rjavat sumetapnā| dundubhau me kāsīkā| ikṣvākuṣu me pittam| saliṅgeṣu me medhyam| aśvataryāṃ me prajasta| puṅścalyāṃ me

pāpavidaḥ| apsu me grataḥ| brahmotye me kilbiṣam|

pāpmaṃ na hāti tamuktvā jahimo vayamanyatrāsma…tāṃ sahasrākṣo amatyo yo no dveṣṭi sariṣyatu yadviṣmastamujahi|
medini devī vasundharā syādvasukṣatu| prāsrat (l2.) bāhu bhuvanasya prajābhyo dhṛtavrato maho ajasya rājasi trirantarikṣaṃ 
savitā mahitvanā| trirajāṃsi paribhūstrīṇi rocanātisre divam pṛthivīstrisra itvati tribhighṛtairahino kṣatitmanā|
siṃhe manyu(l3.) vyāghre me'ntarā mayā vṛke me kṣudaśve meghasirdhanvani me pipāsā| rājagṛhe me śanayāśmāni me tandrīrgardabhe 
me śaśśayate me niraśvame vepathuḥ| kūrme me'ṅgarogo baste (l4.)me'pasar priye me mṛtyurbhrātṛvye me pāpma| sapate me 
niṝtirduṣkīrtau me vṛddhiḥ paraśvati me samṛddhiḥ| khaḍge ma arcirgavaye ma āḍhyagaure me bādhiryamṛkṣe me śoko godhā (l5.) 
yāmme svedo jarāyāṃ me himaḥ| kṛṣṇaśakune me bhirutākaśe me pāpo gandhaḥ ulūke me …bhyaśa….kyoke ma īrṣya utkaṭe me 
duruddhiḥ| kalale me masyā ulale me pradhyā uṣṭre me tṛṣṇā| (p70b., f68a., l1.) ṛśye me śramaḥ| aṅgyāḥ avyāḥ 
kośe me gandhaḥ| kumāryā me'laṅkāraḥ| sūkare me kvadadhuḥ| pradākhuni me svapnā  vidyuti me smayaśaḥ| lobhāyāṃ me kledaḥ| 
śalabhe me pāpmā|
athā(tha) devi vāsavi brahmavarcasaḥ (l2.) pitṛṇāṃ śrotraṃ  cakṣuryaśo manaḥ| devī hiraṇyagarbhiṇī devī prasūdari| sadane satyāya 
ne sida| samudravatī sāvitravatī haro devī mahyaṅge mayā dharaṇī mahāṛ vyādhṛṣṭā śriṅge śriṅge yajñe yajñe (l3.) vibhīṣiṇī| 
indrapatnī vyāpinī surabhiriha vāyumatijala- śayani svayaṃ dhārā…satyandho parimedane| so paridha aṅgāya| viṣṇupatniṃ mahiṃ 
deviṃ mādhaviṃ mādhavapriyām| lakṣmīṃ priyasakhīṃ devīṃ (l4.) namāmyacyutavallabhām| dhanurdharāya vidmahe sarpasidhyai ca dhīmahi 
tanno dharā pracodayāt| iti ṛkkilakārikānukramapañcamāśvāsaḥ|
vāstoṣpadravā sthūṇāmastraṃ saumyānāṃ (l5.)draspo bhettā parā śaśvat| subhayaṃ yadasti| agniryaviṣṭyaḥ praṇudatu tadbhayaṃ śannaḥ 
prajābhyaḥ śamunāya śubhyaḥ a[rki]spandeṅgacale yat| iyaṃ yadvāśmite yaduce- dyaddurikte| agniryaḥ viṣṭhyaḥ + paśubhyaḥ 
(l6.) dusvapne pāpasvapne ca yat| bhayaṃ svapnāśaneyaṃ medhyadarśane| agni….| vāstoṣpate prataraṇo na edhi gayaspāno 
gobhiraśvebhirindo ajarāsaste sakhye syāmapi te (p71a., f68b., l1.) va putrān prati no juṣasva| amīvahā

bhuvaḥ| mṛtyurnaśya + suvaḥ| yāssugandhā rasā varṇā balaṃ ca na hite ubhe| tāḥ āpaḥ śivaḥ santu| (l3.) sukṛtaṃ pravahantu 
me| yā ūrjamahiṣiṃ ca devaprakṣitā mahi| tāme + me| yāsāṃ niṣkramaṇe sarvaṃ yadi majāyate jagat| tāma + medhā 
sāmimetrayerdostejasā yaśasāvṛtāḥ| (l4.) yā dakṣiṇā revatīrāpasravantu + me yāḥ praticī revatīrāprasravaṃ tu …me| yā udīcī 
devarāndhāpa + me| yā ūrjā revatīrāpasravantu| śukrāstāma āpa śivāḥ santu sukṛtaṃ pravahanta me| (l5.) ākvāhanaṃ tu harayaḥ 
sacetasaḥ śvetairaśvaiḥ saha ketumadbhiḥ vātā vairbalavadbhirmanojñavegāyā hi śīghraṃ mama havyāya śarvom| imāṃ āpaḥ śivāḥ śivatamāḥ| 
pūtāḥ pūtatamāḥ …dhyā(l6.) medhyatamā amṛtā amṛtarasāḥ| pādyārghyāstā juṣantām| pratigṛhyatāṃ pratigṛhyatāṃ pratigṛhyatāṃ 
pratigṛhṇātu bhagavan mahādevaḥ| ime gandhāḥ śubhā divyā sarvagandhairalaṅkṛtāḥ| pūtā (p71b., f69a., l1.) brahmapavitreṇa 
pūtā sūryasya raśmibhiḥ| pratigṛhyatāṃ + vaḥ| ime mālyāḥ śubhā divyāḥ sarvamālyairalaṅkṛtāḥ| pūtā brahma + vaḥ| …. puṣpā 
śubhā divyāḥ sarvapuṣpairalaṅkṛtāḥ| pūtā brahmapavitreṇa + raśmabhiḥ| prati + vaḥ| (l2.) vanaspatirasopeto dhūpebhyo dhūpa 
uttamaḥ| āghrāṇaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ dhūpo'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām| prati…jyotiḥ śakraśca tejaśca devānāṃ satataṃ priyaḥ| prakāśaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ 
dīpo'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām| (l3.) prati + vaḥ| tvāmekamādyaṃ puruṣaṃ purātanaṃ rudraṃ śivaṃ viśvasrajaṃ yajāmahe …meva yajñopahite 
vidheyastvamātmātman pratigṛhṇīṣva havyam| ato devā avantu no yato viṣṇurvicakrame| (l4.) pṛthivyāḥ saptadhāmabhiḥ| 
śakunasūktam| umāsūktam| skandasūktam| kumāraṃ mātā skanda…skandṛṣiḥ| skando devatā triṣṭupchandaḥ kumāraṃ mātā 
yuvatiḥ samubdhaṃ guhābibhakti na dadāti pitre| anīkamasya (l5.) na mīna jarjanā saḥ puraḥ paśyanti nihitamaratau||1||
ka me tatvaṃ yuvate kumāraṃ peṣiṣi babharṣi mahiṣi jajānā| pūrvī hi garbhaḥ śarado vavardhā apaśyaṃ jātaṃ yadasūtamātau||2||

gṛhṇantu bali tiṣṭhaṃ tathā kṣayam| asmāka- māyurārogyaṃ priyaṃ ca satataṃ varam| prayaccha dṛkṣavārādidoṣau śledijaṃ namaḥ| 
devatābhyaḥ pitṛbhyaśca mahāyogibhya eva ca|

mṛtyadāridranāśanaṃ śrīpuṣṭikīrtidaṃ vande vipraśrīpādapaṅkajam| vipraughadarśanāt sadyaḥ kriyante pāparaśmayaḥ| vandanānmaṅgalaṃ vāpi 
rarcanādyacyutaṃ (l2.) padam| āgacchatu mahābhāgā viśvedevā- mahābalāḥ| ye atra vihitā śrāddhaṃ sāvadhānā bhavantu te| 
āmāgacchantu pitaro devayānān samudrān salilān su| asmin yajñe sarvakāmān labhante kṣayamāṇa(l3.)mupaduhyantāṃ

gataṃ manaḥ| āyāhi pitaraḥ saumyā gambhīraiḥ patibhiḥ  pūrvaiḥ prajāmasmabhyaṃ dadato rayiṃ ca dīrghāyutvaṃ ca śataśāra(l5.)daṃ ca|

niyatebhiḥ śubhirvājini vā| hṛtapraviṣṭaṃ yadacaraṃ yaviṣṭaṃ tanme + stu| yasmin nṛcaḥ sāmayajūṃṣi yasmin pratiṣṭhā raśanā 
bhāti bhārāḥ| yasmiṃścittaṃ sarvamodaṃ prajānāṃ tanme + stu| (l2.) yadatra ṣaṣṭhaṃ triśataṃ śarīraṃ yajñasya guhyaṃ nava nāma 
vāhyam| daśa pañcatriṃśataṃ yatparaṃ tanme| yaja jāgrato dūramupaitu daivaṃ taduṣuptasya tathaiveti| dūraṃ gama jyotiṣā jyotirekaṃ 
tanme| (l3.) yenedaṃ viśvaṃ jagato babhūva ye devāpi mahato jātavedāḥ| ye devāstamaso jyotirekaṃ tanme| yena dyauḥ pṛthivī 
cāntarikṣaṃ ca ye parvatāḥ pradiśo diśaṃ ca tenedaṃ jagadvyāptaṃ (l4.) prajānāṃ tanme| ye mano hṛdayaṃ ye ca devā ye divyā 
āpo ye sūryaraśmiḥ| te śrotre cakṣuṣi sañcarantaṃ tanme| acintyaṃ cāprameyaṃ ca vyaktāvyaktaparaṃ ca yat| sūkṣmāḥ sūkṣa(kṣma)taraṃ 
jñeyaṃ(l5.) tanme| ye pañca pañcādaśaśataṃ sahasramayutanyarbudaṃ ca te agnicitteṣṭakāstaṃ śarīraṃ tanme| yasyedaṃ dhīrāḥ punanti 
kavayo brahmāṇameta… praṇatamindum| sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ dyaurākāśaṃ tanme| (l6.) parāt parataraṃ caiva tatparāścaiva yat param| 
tat parāt parato jñeyaṃ tanme| yā vedādiṣu gāyatrī sarvavyīpī maheśvarī| ṛgyajussāmādharvaiśca tanme| (p76a., f73b., 
l1.) prayataḥ praṇavoṅkāraṃ praṇavaṃ puruṣottamaḥ| yaḥ sa…..vedaiśca tanme| yo vai devaṃ mahādevaṃ paramaṃ ruruṣottamam oṃkāraṃ 
praṇavātmānaṃ tanme| yo sau varṣeṣu vedeṣu paṭhyate hyacanadrīṃśvaraḥ| (l2.) akāyo nirguṇo hyātmā tanme| gobhirjuṣṭaṃ dhanena 
hyā….balena ca| prajayā paśubhiḥ puṣkarākṣaṃ tanme| caturo vedānadhīyita sa viśāstramayā viduḥ itihāsapurāṇānāṃ tanme| 
kailāsaśikhare ramye (l3.) śaṅkarasya śivālaye| devatāstatra modante tanme| ya idaṃ śivasaṅkalpaṃ sadā dhyāyanti brāhmaṇāḥ| 
te paraṃ mokṣaṃ gamiṣyanti tanme| tamuṣṭu hi yasviṣaḥ sudhanvā yo viśvasya kṣayati bheṣajasya| ikṣvāmahe sau (l4.) manasāya

hi nūṣuṇo balamindrā na ….ksunaḥ| balaṃ tokāya (l5.) tanayāya jīvase| tvaṃ hi baladā asi| śriye jātaśriya āniryāya

pāṣāṇadānam| 
pippalādi samutpanne kṛtyo lokabhayaṅkarī| 
pāṣāṇaṃ te mayā dattamāhārārthaṃ *prakalpitam||* (l2.)
āvāhanam|
viśvācica ghṛtācī ca viśvayone viśāṃ pate| 
sānnidhyaṃ kuru me deva sāgare lavaṇāmbhasi||
namaskāraḥ -
namaste viśvaguptāya namo viṣṇavapāṃ *pate|* (l3.)
namo jaladhirūpāya nadīnāṃ pataye namaḥ||
samudrāya vāyunāya sindhūnāṃ pataye namaḥ|
nadīnāṃ sarvāsāṃ pitre bahudhā viśvakarmaṇe||
viśvāhā martyaṃ haviḥ|(l4.) 
arghyam -
sarvaratnamayaśrīmān sarvaratnākarā karā|
sarvaratnapradhānastvaṃ gṛhāṇārghyaṃ mahodadhe||
prārthanā - 
pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni prāviśastvaṃ mahodadhe|(l5.)
snātavya me phalaṃ dehi sarvasmāttrāhi māṃ sahaḥ||
anujñā -
karāladaṃṣṭravadanakarālābhyatadaśanā|
dehi deva mamānujñāṃ yuṣmattīrthaniṣevaṇe||
atikrūramahākāya kalpāntadahanopama| (p77a., f74b., l1.)
bhairavāya namastubhyamanujñāṃ dātumarhasi||
snānam -
vedādayo vedasiṣṭhayoniḥ saritpatiḥ sāgararatnayoniḥ|
agniśca te teja iḍā ca tejo retodhā viṣṇuramṛtasya nibhiḥ||
idaṃ te anyābhirahamānamadbhiryāḥ *kāñcasindhuṃ praviśamyāpaḥ|* (l2.)
sarpo jīrṇomivatva ca jahāmi pāpaṃ śarīrāt sa śirasko abhyupetya||
tarpaṇam -
pippalādiṃ kavikaṇvaṃ kṛtāntaṃ jīviteśvaram|
manyuṃ ca kālarātriṃ ca ….cāha*rgaṇeśvaram||* (l3.)
kṣetrapiṇḍam -
ābrahmaṇo yo pitṛvaṃśajātāḥ mātustathā vaṃśabhavāmadiryāḥ|
vaṃśadvaye'smi mama dāsabhūtā bhṛtyāstathaivāśritasevakāśca mitrāṇi sakhyam||
paśavaśca *vṛkṣā spṛṣṭāśca dṛṣṭāśca kṛtopakārāḥ|* (l4.)
janmāntaraye mama saṅgatāśca tebhyasvadhā piṇḍamahaṃ dadāmi||1||
pitṛvaṃśe mṛte ye ca mātṛvaṃśe tathaiva ca|
guruśvaśurabandhūnāṃ ye cānye *bāndhavā mṛtāḥ|* (l5.)
ye me kule luptapuṇḍāḥ putradāravivarjitāḥ||
kriyālopahatāścaiva jātyandhāḥ paṅkavastathā|
virūpā āmagarbhaśca jñātāḥ jñā(ajñā)tāḥ kule mama||(p77b., f75a., l1.)
teṣāṃ puṇḍaṃ mayā dattaṃ akṣayyamupatiṣṭhatu|
asipatravane ghore kumbhipāke ca raurave||
teṣāmuddharaṇārthāya idaṃ piṇḍaṃ dadāmyaham|3|
utsannakule koṭīnāṃ yeṣāṃ dātā kule na hi||
dharmapiṇḍaṃ mayā *dattamakṣayyamupatiṣṭhatu|4|* (l2.)
ābrahmastambaparyantaṃ devarṣipitṛmānavāḥ||
tṛpyantu pitaraḥ sarve mātṛmātāmahādayaḥ|5|
atītakulakoṭīnāṃ saptadvīpanivāsinām||
ābrahmabhuvanāllokāt *idamastu tilodakam|* (l3.)
